<a id="source-8dd8f6ff4fb005b4"></a>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_formal_lift_engine.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Reusable one-parameter formal lift engine for the Program 5 Schur model.

Given a first-order tangent vector ``theta`` in the rank-six tangent kernel,
this module recursively constructs

    P(t) = P0 + theta*t + P2*t^2 + P3*t^3 + ...

inside the fixed rank-six Schur chart.  At each order it first solves the
linear image equation, then uses every vector in the 22-dimensional tangent
kernel to remove the next cokernel class.  A failed rank test is accompanied
by an exact left-null certificate.

This is internal research infrastructure.  It audits formal rank-at-most-six
compatibility only; it does not impose the quartic compression equation or
prove convergence/algebraization.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import _q
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import _flatten


def _digest(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; str:
    payload = &#91;str(sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))) for value in vector&#93;
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _nonzero_count(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; int:
    return sum(sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)) != 0 for value in vector)


def _rank_pair(matrix: sp.Matrix, rhs: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;int, int&#93;:
    return (
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rank(),
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
            matrix.row_join(rhs),
            fmt="sparse",
        ).rank(),
    )


def _deterministic_solution(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;bool, sp.Matrix | None, int, int, int&#93;:
    rank, augmented_rank = _rank_pair(matrix, rhs)
    if rank != augmented_rank:
        return False, None, rank, augmented_rank, matrix.cols - rank
    solution_tuple = next(iter(sp.linsolve((matrix, rhs))))
    free_symbols = sorted(
        set().union(*(entry.free_symbols for entry in solution_tuple)),
        key=str,
    )
    substitution = {symbol: 0 for symbol in free_symbols}
    solution = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            sp.factor(sp.cancel(entry.subs(substitution)))
            for entry in solution_tuple
        &#93;
    )
    error = (matrix * solution - rhs).applyfunc(
        lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
    )
    if any(value != 0 for value in error):
        raise AssertionError("deterministic tangent solution failed")
    return True, solution, rank, augmented_rank, len(free_symbols)


def _obstruction_certificate(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
    active_rows: Sequence&#91;int&#93;,
    model: Any,
    *,
    order: int,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    witness = next(
        (
            vector
            for vector in matrix.T.nullspace()
            if sp.factor(sp.cancel((vector.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93;)) != 0
        ),
        None,
    )
    if witness is None:
        raise AssertionError("rank mismatch has no left-null witness")
    first_nonzero = next(value for value in witness if value != 0)
    witness = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            sp.factor(sp.cancel(value / first_nonzero))
            for value in witness
        &#93;
    )
    annihilation = (witness.T * matrix).applyfunc(
        lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
    )
    pairing = sp.factor(sp.cancel((witness.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93;))
    if any(value != 0 for value in annihilation) or pairing == 0:
        raise AssertionError("invalid formal-lift obstruction witness")
    coordinates = &#91;&#93;
    for local_row, coefficient in enumerate(witness):
        if coefficient == 0:
            continue
        coordinates.append(
            {
                "parameter_monomial": f"t^{order}",
                **model.residual_coordinate(active_rows&#91;local_row&#93;),
                "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
            }
        )
    return {
        "kind": "exact left-null witness",
        "order": order,
        "pairing_with_rhs": _q(pairing),
        "nonzero_count": len(coordinates),
        "witness_sha256": _digest(witness),
        "coordinates": coordinates,
    }


def lift_formal_direction(
    model: Any,
    theta: sp.Matrix,
    *,
    name: str,
    max_order: int = 4,
    verify_effect: bool = False,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    """Lift one tangent direction or return its first exact obstruction."""
    if max_order &lt; 2:
        raise ValueError("max_order must be at least two")
    if model.L * theta != sp.zeros(model.L.rows, 1):
        raise AssertionError("first-order direction is not in ker(L)")

    tangent_basis = model.tangent_basis
    theta_block = model.blocks(theta)
    tangent_blocks = &#91;model.blocks(vector) for vector in tangent_basis&#93;
    effect_columns = &#91;
        model.project(
            _flatten(model.bilinear_effect(tangent_block, theta_block))
        )
        for tangent_block in tangent_blocks
    &#93;
    effect_rows = sorted(
        {
            row
            for column in effect_columns
            for row, value in enumerate(column)
            if value != 0
        }
    )
    base_effect_matrix = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            &#91;column&#91;row, 0&#93; for column in effect_columns&#93;
            for row in effect_rows
        &#93;
    )
    fixed_effect_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        base_effect_matrix,
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()

    P_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {1: theta}
    A_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {}
    B_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {0: model.B0}
    C_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {}
    D_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {}
    G_series: dict&#91;int, sp.Matrix&#93; = {0: model.G0}

    def record_blocks(order: int) -&gt; None:
        block = model.blocks(P_series&#91;order&#93;)
        A_series&#91;order&#93; = block&#91;"A"&#93;
        B_series&#91;order&#93; = block&#91;"B"&#93;
        C_series&#91;order&#93; = block&#91;"C"&#93;
        D_series&#91;order&#93; = block&#91;"D"&#93;

    def compute_inverse_order(order: int) -&gt; None:
        total = sp.zeros(6, 6)
        for left_order in range(1, order + 1):
            total += A_series&#91;left_order&#93; * G_series&#91;order - left_order&#93;
        G_series&#91;order&#93; = (-model.G0 * total).applyfunc(
            lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
        )

    def forcing(order: int) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        value = sp.zeros(len(model.zero_rows), len(model.nonpivot_columns))
        for i_order in range(1, order):
            for j_order in range(0, order - i_order + 1):
                k_order = order - i_order - j_order
                if (
                    i_order in C_series
                    and j_order in G_series
                    and k_order in B_series
                ):
                    value += (
                        C_series&#91;i_order&#93;
                        * G_series&#91;j_order&#93;
                        * B_series&#91;k_order&#93;
                    )
        return value.applyfunc(lambda entry: sp.factor(sp.cancel(entry)))

    def tangent_system(
        residual: sp.Matrix,
    ) -&gt; tuple&#91;
        bool,
        sp.Matrix | None,
        int,
        int,
        int,
        list&#91;int&#93;,
        sp.Matrix,
        sp.Matrix,
    &#93;:
        active_rows = sorted(
            set(effect_rows)
            | {row for row, value in enumerate(residual) if value != 0}
        )
        matrix = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                &#91;column&#91;row, 0&#93; for column in effect_columns&#93;
                for row in active_rows
            &#93;
        )
        rhs = sp.Matrix(&#91;-residual&#91;row, 0&#93; for row in active_rows&#93;)
        solvable, solution, rank, augmented_rank, free_dimension = (
            _deterministic_solution(matrix, rhs)
        )
        return (
            solvable,
            solution,
            rank,
            augmented_rank,
            free_dimension,
            active_rows,
            matrix,
            rhs,
        )

    record_blocks(1)
    compute_inverse_order(1)
    H2 = forcing(2)
    residual2 = model.project(_flatten(H2))
    if any(value != 0 for value in residual2):
        return {
            "name": name,
            "maximum_requested_order": max_order,
            "maximum_compatible_order": 1,
            "first_obstructed_order": 2,
            "fixed_tangent_effect_rank": fixed_effect_rank,
            "direct_effect_columns_verified": 0,
            "orders": &#91;
                {"order": 1, "compatible": True},
                {
                    "order": 2,
                    "compatible": False,
                    "obstruction_rank": DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
                        residual2,
                        fmt="sparse",
                    ).rank(),
                },
            &#93;,
        }

    P_series&#91;2&#93; = model.solve_image(H2)
    record_blocks(2)
    compute_inverse_order(2)

    verified_columns = 0
    if verify_effect and max_order &gt;= 3:
        base_residual = model.project(_flatten(forcing(3)))
        original = P_series&#91;2&#93;
        original_blocks = {
            "A": A_series&#91;2&#93;.copy(),
            "B": B_series&#91;2&#93;.copy(),
            "C": C_series&#91;2&#93;.copy(),
            "D": D_series&#91;2&#93;.copy(),
        }
        original_G2 = G_series&#91;2&#93;.copy()
        for tangent_index, tangent_vector in enumerate(tangent_basis):
            P_series&#91;2&#93; = original + tangent_vector
            record_blocks(2)
            compute_inverse_order(2)
            observed = model.project(_flatten(forcing(3))) - base_residual
            observed = observed.applyfunc(
                lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
            )
            expected = effect_columns&#91;tangent_index&#93;
            if observed != expected:
                raise AssertionError(
                    "direct formal effect replay disagrees in tangent column "
                    f"{tangent_index}"
                )
            verified_columns += 1
        P_series&#91;2&#93; = original
        A_series&#91;2&#93; = original_blocks&#91;"A"&#93;
        B_series&#91;2&#93; = original_blocks&#91;"B"&#93;
        C_series&#91;2&#93; = original_blocks&#91;"C"&#93;
        D_series&#91;2&#93; = original_blocks&#91;"D"&#93;
        G_series&#91;2&#93; = original_G2

    orders: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;
        {
            "order": 1,
            "compatible": True,
            "correction_nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(theta),
            "correction_sha256": _digest(theta),
        },
        {
            "order": 2,
            "compatible": True,
            "provisional_correction_nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(P_series&#91;2&#93;),
            "provisional_correction_sha256": _digest(P_series&#91;2&#93;),
        },
    &#93;
    first_obstructed_order: int | None = None
    obstruction: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None

    for order in range(3, max_order + 1):
        residual = model.project(_flatten(forcing(order)))
        (
            solvable,
            solution,
            rank,
            augmented_rank,
            free_dimension,
            active_rows,
            matrix,
            rhs,
        ) = tangent_system(residual)
        if not solvable or solution is None:
            first_obstructed_order = order
            obstruction = _obstruction_certificate(
                matrix,
                rhs,
                active_rows,
                model,
                order=order,
            )
            orders.append(
                {
                    "order": order,
                    "compatible": False,
                    "effect_rank": rank,
                    "augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
                    "obstruction_certificate": obstruction,
                }
            )
            break

        adjustment = sp.zeros(model.ambient_operation_dimension, 1)
        adjustment_coefficients = &#91;&#93;
        for tangent_index, tangent_vector in enumerate(tangent_basis):
            coefficient = solution&#91;tangent_index, 0&#93;
            if coefficient:
                adjustment += coefficient * tangent_vector
                adjustment_coefficients.append(
                    {
                        "tangent_basis_index": tangent_index,
                        "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                    }
                )
        P_series&#91;order - 1&#93; += adjustment
        record_blocks(order - 1)
        compute_inverse_order(order - 1)

        adjusted_forcing = forcing(order)
        adjusted_residual = model.project(_flatten(adjusted_forcing))
        if any(value != 0 for value in adjusted_residual):
            raise AssertionError(
                f"the order-{order} tangent adjustment left a residual"
            )
        P_series&#91;order&#93; = model.solve_image(adjusted_forcing)
        record_blocks(order)
        compute_inverse_order(order)

        orders&#91;-1&#93;&#91;"finalized_correction_nonzero_count"&#93; = _nonzero_count(
            P_series&#91;order - 1&#93;
        )
        orders&#91;-1&#93;&#91;"finalized_correction_sha256"&#93; = _digest(
            P_series&#91;order - 1&#93;
        )
        orders&#91;-1&#93;&#91;"tangent_adjustment_coefficient_count"&#93; = len(
            adjustment_coefficients
        )
        orders&#91;-1&#93;&#91;"tangent_adjustment_coefficients"&#93; = adjustment_coefficients
        orders.append(
            {
                "order": order,
                "compatible": True,
                "effect_rank": rank,
                "augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
                "tangent_solution_free_dimension": free_dimension,
                "provisional_correction_nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(
                    P_series&#91;order&#93;
                ),
                "provisional_correction_sha256": _digest(P_series&#91;order&#93;),
            }
        )

    compatible_orders = &#91;item&#91;"order"&#93; for item in orders if item&#91;"compatible"&#93;&#93;
    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "name": name,
        "maximum_requested_order": max_order,
        "maximum_compatible_order": max(compatible_orders),
        "first_obstructed_order": first_obstructed_order,
        "fixed_tangent_effect_rank": fixed_effect_rank,
        "direct_effect_columns_verified": verified_columns,
        "orders": orders,
    }
    if obstruction is not None:
        result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93; = obstruction
    return result
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_fourth_order_kuranishi.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compute the intrinsic fourth-order Kuranishi map for ``theta_u``.

The first-order direction

    theta_u = eta_0 + xi_4

has a solvable cubic lifting equation.  That equation has rank two on the
22-dimensional tangent kernel, so its quadratic correction is not unique: it
has a 20-dimensional affine family.  A quartic failure obtained after setting
those twenty free parameters to zero is therefore only branch-specific.

This adapter retains the complete cubic-lift fibre.  It constructs a
particular quadratic correction, a basis N_0,...,N_19 of the homogeneous
cubic-compatible quadratic corrections, and the exact degree-at-most-two map

    kappa_4(z_0,...,z_19)

obtained by projecting the fourth-order forcing modulo both the rank-six
tangent image and the order-three tangent-effect image.  It also searches for
a rational zero of the displayed polynomial system.

This is a formal local rank-at-most-six calculation.  It does not impose the
quartic compression functional, classify the full infinity first-order fibre,
or prove convergence/algebraization.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import DEFAULT_SOURCE, _q
from .program5_rank_six_schur_model import build_schur_model
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import (
    _flatten,
    _independent_rows_and_columns,
)


def _digest(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; str:
    payload = &#91;str(sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))) for value in vector&#93;
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _nonzero_count(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; int:
    return sum(sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)) != 0 for value in vector)


def _deterministic_solution(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Matrix, int&#93;:
    solution_tuple = next(iter(sp.linsolve((matrix, rhs))))
    free_symbols = sorted(
        set().union(*(entry.free_symbols for entry in solution_tuple)),
        key=str,
    )
    substitution = {symbol: 0 for symbol in free_symbols}
    solution = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            sp.factor(sp.cancel(entry.subs(substitution)))
            for entry in solution_tuple
        &#93;
    )
    error = (matrix * solution - rhs).applyfunc(
        lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
    )
    if any(value != 0 for value in error):
        raise AssertionError("deterministic affine solution failed")
    return solution, len(free_symbols)


def _project_modulo_image(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Matrix, tuple&#91;int, ...&#93;, tuple&#91;int, ...&#93;&#93;:
    pivot_rows, pivot_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(matrix)
    minor = matrix&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    inverse = minor.inv()
    coefficients = inverse * vector&#91;list(pivot_rows), :&#93;
    residual = (
        vector - matrix&#91;:, list(pivot_columns)&#93; * coefficients
    ).applyfunc(lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)))
    if any(residual&#91;row, 0&#93; != 0 for row in pivot_rows):
        raise AssertionError("secondary cokernel projection kept a pivot row")
    return residual, tuple(pivot_rows), tuple(pivot_columns)


def _polynomial_terms(
    expression: sp.Expr,
    variables: Sequence&#91;sp.Symbol&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *variables, domain=sp.QQ)
    terms = &#91;&#93;
    for exponents, coefficient in polynomial.terms():
        monomial_factors = &#91;&#93;
        for variable, exponent in zip(variables, exponents):
            if exponent == 1:
                monomial_factors.append(str(variable))
            elif exponent &gt; 1:
                monomial_factors.append(f"{variable}^{exponent}")
        terms.append(
            {
                "monomial": "*".join(monomial_factors) or "1",
                "exponents": list(exponents),
                "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
            }
        )
    return terms


def _rational_single_coordinate_solution(
    equations: Sequence&#91;sp.Expr&#93;,
    variables: Sequence&#91;sp.Symbol&#93;,
) -&gt; dict&#91;sp.Symbol, sp.Rational&#93; | None:
    t = sp.symbols("t")
    zero_substitution = {variable: 0 for variable in variables}
    if all(sp.factor(expression.subs(zero_substitution)) == 0 for expression in equations):
        return {variable: sp.Rational(0) for variable in variables}

    for variable in variables:
        substitution = {
            other: (t if other == variable else 0)
            for other in variables
        }
        polynomials = &#91;
            sp.Poly(
                sp.factor(expression.subs(substitution)),
                t,
                domain=sp.QQ,
            )
            for expression in equations
        &#93;
        nonzero = &#91;polynomial for polynomial in polynomials if not polynomial.is_zero&#93;
        if not nonzero:
            return {
                other: (sp.Rational(1) if other == variable else sp.Rational(0))
                for other in variables
            }
        common = nonzero&#91;0&#93;
        for polynomial in nonzero&#91;1:&#93;:
            common = sp.gcd(common, polynomial)
        for root in sp.roots(common.as_expr(), t):
            if root.is_Rational:
                candidate = {
                    other: (sp.Rational(root) if other == variable else sp.Rational(0))
                    for other in variables
                }
                if all(
                    sp.factor(expression.subs(candidate)) == 0
                    for expression in equations
                ):
                    return candidate

    for fixed_index, fixed_variable in enumerate(variables):
        for fixed_value in (sp.Rational(1), sp.Rational(-1)):
            for solved_variable in variables&#91;fixed_index + 1 :&#93;:
                substitution = {
                    variable: (
                        fixed_value
                        if variable == fixed_variable
                        else (t if variable == solved_variable else 0)
                    )
                    for variable in variables
                }
                polynomials = &#91;
                    sp.Poly(
                        sp.factor(expression.subs(substitution)),
                        t,
                        domain=sp.QQ,
                    )
                    for expression in equations
                &#93;
                nonzero = &#91;
                    polynomial
                    for polynomial in polynomials
                    if not polynomial.is_zero
                &#93;
                if not nonzero:
                    candidate = {
                        variable: (
                            fixed_value
                            if variable == fixed_variable
                            else (
                                sp.Rational(0)
                                if variable != solved_variable
                                else sp.Rational(0)
                            )
                        )
                        for variable in variables
                    }
                    return candidate
                common = nonzero&#91;0&#93;
                for polynomial in nonzero&#91;1:&#93;:
                    common = sp.gcd(common, polynomial)
                for root in sp.roots(common.as_expr(), t):
                    if root.is_Rational:
                        candidate = {
                            variable: (
                                fixed_value
                                if variable == fixed_variable
                                else (
                                    sp.Rational(root)
                                    if variable == solved_variable
                                    else sp.Rational(0)
                                )
                            )
                            for variable in variables
                        }
                        if all(
                            sp.factor(expression.subs(candidate)) == 0
                            for expression in equations
                        ):
                            return candidate
    return None


def analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi(
    source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    model = build_schur_model(source_path)
    theta = model.theta_u
    theta_block = model.blocks(theta)
    tangent_matrix = sp.Matrix.hstack(*model.tangent_basis)
    tangent_blocks = &#91;model.blocks(vector) for vector in model.tangent_basis&#93;

    H2 = model.quadratic_forcing(theta_block)
    if any(value != 0 for value in model.project(_flatten(H2))):
        raise AssertionError("theta_u lost second-order compatibility")
    P2_image_particular = model.solve_image(H2)

    cubic_residual = model.project(
        _flatten(
            model.cubic_forcing(
                theta_block,
                model.blocks(P2_image_particular),
            )
        )
    )
    effect_columns = &#91;
        model.project(
            _flatten(model.bilinear_effect(tangent_block, theta_block))
        )
        for tangent_block in tangent_blocks
    &#93;
    effect_matrix = sp.Matrix.hstack(*effect_columns)
    effect_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        effect_matrix,
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()
    augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        effect_matrix.row_join(-cubic_residual),
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()
    if effect_rank != augmented_rank:
        raise AssertionError("theta_u cubic lift became obstructed")

    tangent_coordinates, reported_free_dimension = _deterministic_solution(
        effect_matrix,
        -cubic_residual,
    )
    tangent_particular = tangent_matrix * tangent_coordinates
    P2_base = P2_image_particular + tangent_particular
    cubic_check = model.project(
        _flatten(model.cubic_forcing(theta_block, model.blocks(P2_base)))
    )
    if any(value != 0 for value in cubic_check):
        raise AssertionError("particular quadratic correction did not kill cubic forcing")

    kernel_coordinates = effect_matrix.nullspace()
    if len(kernel_coordinates) != len(model.tangent_basis) - effect_rank:
        raise AssertionError("cubic-effect nullity changed")
    if reported_free_dimension != len(kernel_coordinates):
        raise AssertionError("linsolve free dimension disagrees with nullspace")
    homogeneous_directions = &#91;
        tangent_matrix * coordinates
        for coordinates in kernel_coordinates
    &#93;

    _, effect_pivot_rows, effect_pivot_columns = _project_modulo_image(
        sp.zeros(effect_matrix.rows, 1),
        effect_matrix,
    )
    effect_minor = effect_matrix&#91;effect_pivot_rows, effect_pivot_columns&#93;
    effect_minor_inverse = effect_minor.inv()

    def project_effect(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        coefficients = effect_minor_inverse * vector&#91;list(effect_pivot_rows), :&#93;
        residual = (
            vector
            - effect_matrix&#91;:, list(effect_pivot_columns)&#93; * coefficients
        ).applyfunc(lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)))
        if any(residual&#91;row, 0&#93; != 0 for row in effect_pivot_rows):
            raise AssertionError("order-three cokernel projection kept a pivot row")
        return residual

    def forcing_through_four(P2: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        block1 = theta_block
        block2 = model.blocks(P2)
        G0 = model.G0
        G1 = -G0 * block1&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        G2 = -G0 * (
            block1&#91;"A"&#93; * G1 + block2&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        )

        H3 = (
            block1&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * block2&#91;"B"&#93;
            + block1&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * block1&#91;"B"&#93;
            + block1&#91;"C"&#93; * G2 * model.B0
            + block2&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * block1&#91;"B"&#93;
            + block2&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * model.B0
        ).applyfunc(lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)))
        if any(value != 0 for value in model.project(_flatten(H3))):
            raise AssertionError("quadratic correction left cubic forcing")
        P3 = model.solve_image(H3)
        block3 = model.blocks(P3)
        G3 = -G0 * (
            block1&#91;"A"&#93; * G2
            + block2&#91;"A"&#93; * G1
            + block3&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        )

        blocks = {1: block1, 2: block2, 3: block3}
        inverses = {0: G0, 1: G1, 2: G2, 3: G3}
        B_series = {0: model.B0, 1: block1&#91;"B"&#93;, 2: block2&#91;"B"&#93;, 3: block3&#91;"B"&#93;}
        H4 = sp.zeros(len(model.zero_rows), len(model.nonpivot_columns))
        for i_order in range(1, 4):
            for j_order in range(0, 4 - i_order + 1):
                k_order = 4 - i_order - j_order
                if k_order in B_series:
                    H4 += (
                        blocks&#91;i_order&#93;&#91;"C"&#93;
                        * inverses&#91;j_order&#93;
                        * B_series&#91;k_order&#93;
                    )
        residual4 = model.project(_flatten(H4))
        return project_effect(residual4)

    q0 = forcing_through_four(P2_base)
    plus_values = &#91;&#93;
    minus_values = &#91;&#93;
    linear_coefficients = &#91;&#93;
    diagonal_coefficients = &#91;&#93;
    for direction in homogeneous_directions:
        plus = forcing_through_four(P2_base + direction)
        minus = forcing_through_four(P2_base - direction)
        plus_values.append(plus)
        minus_values.append(minus)
        linear_coefficients.append(
            ((plus - minus) / 2).applyfunc(
                lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
            )
        )
        diagonal_coefficients.append(
            ((plus + minus - 2 * q0) / 2).applyfunc(
                lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
            )
        )

    off_diagonal_coefficients: dict&#91;tuple&#91;int, int&#93;, sp.Matrix&#93; = {}
    for left in range(len(homogeneous_directions)):
        for right in range(left + 1, len(homogeneous_directions)):
            value = forcing_through_four(
                P2_base
                + homogeneous_directions&#91;left&#93;
                + homogeneous_directions&#91;right&#93;
            )
            off_diagonal_coefficients&#91;(left, right)&#93; = (
                value - plus_values&#91;left&#93; - plus_values&#91;right&#93; + q0
            ).applyfunc(lambda entry: sp.factor(sp.cancel(entry)))

    coefficient_vectors = &#91;q0&#93;
    coefficient_labels = &#91;"1"&#93;
    coefficient_vectors.extend(linear_coefficients)
    coefficient_labels.extend(
        f"z_{index}" for index in range(len(homogeneous_directions))
    )
    coefficient_vectors.extend(diagonal_coefficients)
    coefficient_labels.extend(
        f"z_{index}^2" for index in range(len(homogeneous_directions))
    )
    for (left, right), vector in off_diagonal_coefficients.items():
        coefficient_vectors.append(vector)
        coefficient_labels.append(f"z_{left}*z_{right}")

    coefficient_matrix = sp.Matrix.hstack(*coefficient_vectors)
    coefficient_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        coefficient_matrix,
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()
    _, independent_equation_rows = coefficient_matrix.T.rref()
    independent_equation_rows = list(independent_equation_rows)
    variables = sp.symbols(f"z0:{len(homogeneous_directions)}")
    equations = &#91;&#93;
    for row in independent_equation_rows:
        expression = q0&#91;row, 0&#93;
        for index, vector in enumerate(linear_coefficients):
            expression += vector&#91;row, 0&#93; * variables&#91;index&#93;
        for index, vector in enumerate(diagonal_coefficients):
            expression += vector&#91;row, 0&#93; * variables&#91;index&#93; ** 2
        for (left, right), vector in off_diagonal_coefficients.items():
            expression += vector&#91;row, 0&#93; * variables&#91;left&#93; * variables&#91;right&#93;
        expression = sp.factor(expression)
        coordinate = model.residual_coordinate(row)
        equations.append(
            {
                "residual_row": row,
                **coordinate,
                "expression": str(expression),
                "total_degree": sp.Poly(expression, *variables).total_degree(),
                "terms": _polynomial_terms(expression, variables),
            }
        )

    equation_expressions = &#91;
        sp.sympify(equation&#91;"expression"&#93;, locals={str(v): v for v in variables})
        for equation in equations
    &#93;
    rational_solution = _rational_single_coordinate_solution(
        equation_expressions,
        variables,
    )
    solution_record = None
    verified_rational_zero = False
    if rational_solution is not None:
        verified_rational_zero = all(
            sp.factor(expression.subs(rational_solution)) == 0
            for expression in equation_expressions
        )
        if not verified_rational_zero:
            raise AssertionError("reported rational quartic zero did not verify")
        free_adjustment = sp.zeros(model.ambient_operation_dimension, 1)
        coefficients = &#91;&#93;
        for index, variable in enumerate(variables):
            coefficient = rational_solution&#91;variable&#93;
            if coefficient:
                free_adjustment += coefficient * homogeneous_directions&#91;index&#93;
                coefficients.append(
                    {
                        "cubic_fibre_basis_index": index,
                        "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                    }
                )
        P2_solution = P2_base + free_adjustment
        residual_solution = forcing_through_four(P2_solution)
        if any(value != 0 for value in residual_solution):
            raise AssertionError("rational zero left a quartic residual")
        solution_record = {
            "coefficient_count": len(coefficients),
            "coefficients": coefficients,
            "free_adjustment_nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(free_adjustment),
            "free_adjustment_sha256": _digest(free_adjustment),
            "quadratic_correction_nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(P2_solution),
            "quadratic_correction_sha256": _digest(P2_solution),
        }

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": "Program 5 intrinsic fourth-order Kuranishi map at theta_u",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": model.source_sha256,
        "first_order_direction": "theta_u = eta_0 + xi_4",
        "ambient_operation_dimension": model.ambient_operation_dimension,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": len(model.tangent_basis),
        "cubic_effect_rank": effect_rank,
        "cubic_augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
        "cubic_lift_affine_dimension": len(kernel_coordinates),
        "quadratic_image_particular": {
            "nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(P2_image_particular),
            "sha256": _digest(P2_image_particular),
        },
        "quadratic_tangent_particular": {
            "nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(tangent_particular),
            "sha256": _digest(tangent_particular),
        },
        "deterministic_quartic_residual": {
            "nonzero_count": _nonzero_count(q0),
            "sha256": _digest(q0),
        },
        "quartic_kuranishi": {
            "source_dimension": len(homogeneous_directions),
            "target_coefficient_rank": coefficient_rank,
            "independent_equation_count": len(equations),
            "coefficient_label_count": len(coefficient_labels),
            "equations": equations,
        },
        "rational_zero_found": rational_solution is not None,
        "rational_zero_verified": verified_rational_zero,
        "rational_zero": solution_record,
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "The map saturates every quadratic correction compatible with "
            "the cubic equation for theta_u. A zero lifts this one first-order "
            "direction through order four; a nonzero map without a solved zero "
            "is not by itself an obstruction theorem over an algebraic closure."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(
            f"program5_rank_six_fourth_order_kuranishi: {exc}",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

<a id="source-25db7769f82b5f67"></a>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_generic_line_obstruction.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compute the cubic obstruction for every finite line in the selected plane.

Write the finite projective tangent direction as

    theta(r) = theta_v + r theta_u.

The quadratic Kuranishi class vanishes identically on this plane.  This module
constructs, over Q(r), the cubic tangent-effect matrix E(r) and deterministic
cubic residual b(r).  It computes a primitive polynomial basis of the left
kernel of E(r), then studies the ideal in Q&#91;r&#93; generated by all pairings

    w_i(r)^T b(r).

If those pairings generate the unit ideal, a polynomial Bezout combination of
the left-null vectors has constant nonzero pairing with b(r).  That gives one
exact obstruction certificate valid for every finite algebraic value of r.
If the pairing ideal is proper, its factors are reported as the only possible
exceptional finite slopes; each would still require a specialized rank audit.

The point at infinity theta_u is excluded and is audited separately by
``program5_rank_six_line_formal_arc.py``.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import DEFAULT_SOURCE, _q
from .program5_rank_six_schur_model import build_schur_model
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import _flatten


def _digest(expressions: Sequence&#91;sp.Expr&#93;) -&gt; str:
    payload = &#91;str(sp.factor(expression)) for expression in expressions&#93;
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _global_coefficient_denominator(
    expressions: Sequence&#91;sp.Expr&#93;,
    variable: sp.Symbol,
) -&gt; int:
    denominator = 1
    for expression in expressions:
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, variable, domain=sp.QQ)
        for coefficient in polynomial.all_coeffs():
            denominator = sp.ilcm(
                denominator,
                int(sp.denom(coefficient)),
            )
    return denominator


def _primitive_polynomial_vector(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    variable: sp.Symbol,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Matrix, sp.Expr, sp.Expr&#93;:
    fractions = &#91;sp.cancel(value) for value in vector&#93;
    denominator_lcm: sp.Expr = sp.Integer(1)
    for value in fractions:
        denominator_lcm = sp.lcm(
            denominator_lcm,
            sp.denom(value),
        )
    polynomial_entries = &#91;
        sp.Poly(
            sp.cancel(value * denominator_lcm),
            variable,
            domain=sp.QQ,
        ).as_expr()
        for value in fractions
    &#93;
    nonzero_polynomials = &#91;
        sp.Poly(value, variable, domain=sp.QQ)
        for value in polynomial_entries
        if value != 0
    &#93;
    if not nonzero_polynomials:
        raise AssertionError("zero left-kernel vector")
    common = nonzero_polynomials&#91;0&#93;
    for polynomial in nonzero_polynomials&#91;1:&#93;:
        common = sp.gcd(common, polynomial)
    common_expression = common.monic().as_expr()
    polynomial_entries = &#91;
        sp.cancel(value / common_expression)
        for value in polynomial_entries
    &#93;
    coefficient_denominator = _global_coefficient_denominator(
        polynomial_entries,
        variable,
    )
    polynomial_entries = &#91;
        sp.expand(coefficient_denominator * value)
        for value in polynomial_entries
    &#93;
    integer_coefficients = &#91;&#93;
    for value in polynomial_entries:
        integer_coefficients.extend(
            int(coefficient)
            for coefficient in sp.Poly(value, variable, domain=sp.ZZ).all_coeffs()
        )
    content = 0
    for coefficient in integer_coefficients:
        content = sp.igcd(content, abs(coefficient))
    if content &gt; 1:
        polynomial_entries = &#91;
            sp.expand(value / content)
            for value in polynomial_entries
        &#93;
    first_nonzero = next(value for value in polynomial_entries if value != 0)
    if sp.Poly(first_nonzero, variable, domain=sp.QQ).LC() &lt; 0:
        polynomial_entries = &#91;-value for value in polynomial_entries&#93;
    primitive = sp.Matrix(polynomial_entries)

    primitive_nonzero = &#91;
        sp.Poly(value, variable, domain=sp.QQ)
        for value in primitive
        if value != 0
    &#93;
    primitive_gcd = primitive_nonzero&#91;0&#93;
    for polynomial in primitive_nonzero&#91;1:&#93;:
        primitive_gcd = sp.gcd(primitive_gcd, polynomial)
    return primitive, sp.factor(denominator_lcm), primitive_gcd.monic().as_expr()


def _bezout_pairing_ideal(
    pairings: Sequence&#91;sp.Expr&#93;,
    variable: sp.Symbol,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Poly, list&#91;sp.Poly&#93;&#93;:
    nonzero = &#91;
        (index, sp.Poly(pairing, variable, domain=sp.QQ))
        for index, pairing in enumerate(pairings)
        if pairing != 0
    &#93;
    if not nonzero:
        raise AssertionError("every left-null pairing vanished identically")

    first_index, generator = nonzero&#91;0&#93;
    coefficients = &#91;sp.Poly(0, variable, domain=sp.QQ) for _ in pairings&#93;
    coefficients&#91;first_index&#93; = sp.Poly(1, variable, domain=sp.QQ)

    for index, polynomial in nonzero&#91;1:&#93;:
        left_coefficient, right_coefficient, new_generator = sp.gcdex(
            generator,
            polynomial,
        )
        coefficients = &#91;
            left_coefficient * coefficient
            for coefficient in coefficients
        &#93;
        coefficients&#91;index&#93; += right_coefficient
        generator = new_generator

    if generator.is_zero:
        raise AssertionError("Bezout computation returned the zero generator")
    leading = generator.LC()
    generator = generator.monic()
    coefficients = &#91;
        sp.Poly(
            coefficient.as_expr() / leading,
            variable,
            domain=sp.QQ,
        )
        for coefficient in coefficients
    &#93;
    verification = sp.Poly(0, variable, domain=sp.QQ)
    for coefficient, pairing in zip(coefficients, pairings):
        verification += coefficient * sp.Poly(
            pairing,
            variable,
            domain=sp.QQ,
        )
    if verification != generator:
        raise AssertionError("Bezout coefficients do not reproduce the pairing gcd")
    return generator, coefficients


def _witness_coordinates(
    witness: sp.Matrix,
    active_rows: Sequence&#91;int&#93;,
    model: Any,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    coordinates = &#91;&#93;
    for local_row, coefficient in enumerate(witness):
        if coefficient == 0:
            continue
        coordinate = model.residual_coordinate(active_rows&#91;local_row&#93;)
        coordinates.append(
            {
                **coordinate,
                "coefficient_polynomial": str(sp.factor(coefficient)),
            }
        )
    return coordinates


def analyze_generic_line_obstruction(
    source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    model = build_schur_model(source_path)
    r = sp.symbols("r")
    theta = model.theta_v + r * model.theta_u
    theta_block = model.blocks(theta)

    H2 = model.quadratic_forcing(theta_block)
    forcing2 = _flatten(H2)
    residual2 = model.project(forcing2)
    if any(value != 0 for value in residual2):
        raise AssertionError("the selected plane lost second-order compatibility")
    P2 = model.solve_image(H2)

    P2_block = model.blocks(P2)
    cubic_residual = model.project(
        _flatten(model.cubic_forcing(theta_block, P2_block))
    )
    tangent_blocks = &#91;model.blocks(vector) for vector in model.tangent_basis&#93;
    effect_columns = &#91;
        model.project(
            _flatten(model.bilinear_effect(tangent_block, theta_block))
        )
        for tangent_block in tangent_blocks
    &#93;
    active_rows = sorted(
        {
            row
            for row, value in enumerate(cubic_residual)
            if value != 0
        }
        | {
            row
            for column in effect_columns
            for row, value in enumerate(column)
            if value != 0
        }
    )
    effect_matrix = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            &#91;column&#91;row, 0&#93; for column in effect_columns&#93;
            for row in active_rows
        &#93;
    )
    residual_vector = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;cubic_residual&#91;row, 0&#93; for row in active_rows&#93;
    )

    effect_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        effect_matrix,
        fmt="sparse",
    ).to_field().rank()
    augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        effect_matrix.row_join(residual_vector),
        fmt="sparse",
    ).to_field().rank()
    left_kernel = effect_matrix.T.nullspace()
    if len(left_kernel) != len(active_rows) - effect_rank:
        raise AssertionError("symbolic left-nullity disagrees with rank")
    if not left_kernel:
        raise AssertionError("generic cubic effect matrix has no left kernel")

    primitive_witnesses: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    witness_records: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    pairings: list&#91;sp.Expr&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for index, rational_witness in enumerate(left_kernel):
        witness, cleared_denominator, coordinate_gcd = (
            _primitive_polynomial_vector(rational_witness, r)
        )
        annihilation = (witness.T * effect_matrix).applyfunc(
            lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
        )
        if any(value != 0 for value in annihilation):
            raise AssertionError(
                f"primitive witness {index} does not annihilate E(r)"
            )
        pairing = sp.factor(
            sp.cancel((witness.T * residual_vector)&#91;0, 0&#93;)
        )
        sp.Poly(pairing, r, domain=sp.QQ)
        coordinates = _witness_coordinates(witness, active_rows, model)
        primitive_witnesses.append(witness)
        pairings.append(pairing)
        witness_records.append(
            {
                "index": index,
                "cleared_denominator": str(cleared_denominator),
                "coordinate_gcd": str(sp.factor(coordinate_gcd)),
                "nonzero_count": len(coordinates),
                "sha256": _digest(list(witness)),
                "pairing_with_cubic_residual": str(pairing),
                "pairing_degree": sp.Poly(
                    pairing,
                    r,
                    domain=sp.QQ,
                ).degree(),
                "coordinates": coordinates,
            }
        )

    pairing_generator, bezout_coefficients = _bezout_pairing_ideal(pairings, r)
    combined = sp.zeros(len(active_rows), 1)
    for coefficient, witness in zip(bezout_coefficients, primitive_witnesses):
        combined += coefficient.as_expr() * witness
    combined, combined_denominator, combined_coordinate_gcd = (
        _primitive_polynomial_vector(combined, r)
    )
    combined_annihilation = (combined.T * effect_matrix).applyfunc(
        lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
    )
    if any(value != 0 for value in combined_annihilation):
        raise AssertionError("combined Bezout witness does not annihilate E(r)")
    combined_pairing = sp.factor(
        sp.cancel((combined.T * residual_vector)&#91;0, 0&#93;)
    )
    combined_pairing_polynomial = sp.Poly(
        combined_pairing,
        r,
        domain=sp.QQ,
    )
    generator_expression = sp.factor(pairing_generator.as_expr())
    universal = pairing_generator.degree() == 0 and combined_pairing != 0

    factor_records = &#91;
        {
            "polynomial": str(sp.factor(factor.as_expr())),
            "degree": factor.degree(),
            "multiplicity": multiplicity,
        }
        for factor, multiplicity in sp.factor_list(pairing_generator)&#91;1&#93;
    &#93;
    combined_coordinates = _witness_coordinates(combined, active_rows, model)

    sample_checks = &#91;&#93;
    for value in (-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        substitution = {r: sp.Rational(value)}
        specialized_matrix = effect_matrix.subs(substitution)
        specialized_residual = residual_vector.subs(substitution)
        specialized_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
            specialized_matrix,
            fmt="sparse",
        ).rank()
        specialized_augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
            specialized_matrix.row_join(specialized_residual),
            fmt="sparse",
        ).rank()
        specialized_witness = combined.subs(substitution)
        specialized_pairing = sp.factor(combined_pairing.subs(substitution))
        if universal:
            if specialized_witness == sp.zeros(combined.rows, 1):
                raise AssertionError(f"universal witness vanished at r={value}")
            if specialized_pairing == 0:
                raise AssertionError(f"universal pairing vanished at r={value}")
        sample_checks.append(
            {
                "u_over_v": value,
                "effect_rank": specialized_rank,
                "augmented_rank": specialized_augmented_rank,
                "cubic_lift_solvable": (
                    specialized_rank == specialized_augmented_rank
                ),
                "combined_witness_nonzero_count": sum(
                    entry != 0 for entry in specialized_witness
                ),
                "combined_pairing": _q(specialized_pairing),
            }
        )

    return {
        "schema_version": 2,
        "name": "Program 5 symbolic finite-ratio cubic obstruction",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": model.source_sha256,
        "parameter": "r=u/v",
        "direction": "theta_v + r*theta_u",
        "ambient_operation_dimension": model.ambient_operation_dimension,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": len(model.tangent_basis),
        "second_order_residual_identically_zero": True,
        "compressed_cubic_equation_count": len(active_rows),
        "generic_effect_rank_over_Q(r)": effect_rank,
        "generic_augmented_rank_over_Q(r)": augmented_rank,
        "left_kernel_dimension_over_Q(r)": len(left_kernel),
        "primitive_left_kernel_basis": witness_records,
        "pairing_ideal": {
            "generator": str(generator_expression),
            "degree": pairing_generator.degree(),
            "factorization": factor_records,
            "bezout_coefficients": &#91;
                str(sp.factor(coefficient.as_expr()))
                for coefficient in bezout_coefficients
            &#93;,
        },
        "combined_bezout_certificate": {
            "cleared_denominator": str(combined_denominator),
            "coordinate_gcd": str(sp.factor(combined_coordinate_gcd)),
            "nonzero_count": len(combined_coordinates),
            "sha256": _digest(list(combined)),
            "pairing_with_cubic_residual": str(combined_pairing),
            "pairing_degree": combined_pairing_polynomial.degree(),
            "coordinates": combined_coordinates,
        },
        "universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios": universal,
        "sample_checks": sample_checks,
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "The certificate concerns all finite lines in this selected "
            "second-order-compatible tangent plane. It does not classify "
            "directions outside the plane or the point at infinity theta_u."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_generic_line_obstruction(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(
            f"program5_rank_six_generic_line_obstruction: {exc}",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_infinity_fibre_formal_scan.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Lift coordinate samples of the Program 5 infinity row-base fibre.

At the projective point v=0, u=1, quadratic compatibility fixes

    s3 = 0,  s4 = 1,

and leaves the other eighteen row-zero tangent coordinates free.  The base
section theta_u survives cubic order but is obstructed at order four.  This
adapter tests theta_u and each positive coordinate translate

    theta_u + xi_i,  i not in {3,4},

through order four, always allowing the complete 22-dimensional tangent
freedom in every higher correction.

This coordinate scan is exploratory.  It does not classify the full
18-dimensional infinity fibre; surviving samples are branch candidates for a
subsequent higher-order or symbolic family audit.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

from .program5_compression_export import DEFAULT_SOURCE
from .program5_rank_six_formal_lift_engine import lift_formal_direction
from .program5_rank_six_schur_model import build_schur_model


def analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan(
    source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    *,
    max_order: int = 4,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    model = build_schur_model(source_path)
    infinity_indices = &#91;index for index in range(20) if index not in (3, 4)&#93;
    samples = &#91;
        {
            "name": "infinity:base",
            "row_adjustments": &#91;&#93;,
            "theta": model.theta_u,
        }
    &#93; + &#91;
        {
            "name": f"infinity:+xi_{index}",
            "row_adjustments": &#91;
                {"row_basis_index": index, "coefficient": 1}
            &#93;,
            "theta": model.theta_u + model.row_basis&#91;index&#93;,
        }
        for index in infinity_indices
    &#93;

    results = &#91;&#93;
    for sample in samples:
        lift = lift_formal_direction(
            model,
            sample&#91;"theta"&#93;,
            name=sample&#91;"name"&#93;,
            max_order=max_order,
            verify_effect=sample&#91;"name"&#93; == "infinity:base",
        )
        results.append(
            {
                "name": sample&#91;"name"&#93;,
                "row_adjustments": sample&#91;"row_adjustments"&#93;,
                **lift,
            }
        )

    first_obstruction_histogram: dict&#91;str, int&#93; = {}
    survivors = &#91;&#93;
    for result in results:
        obstruction = result&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;
        key = "none" if obstruction is None else str(obstruction)
        first_obstruction_histogram&#91;key&#93; = (
            first_obstruction_histogram.get(key, 0) + 1
        )
        if obstruction is None:
            survivors.append(result&#91;"name"&#93;)

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": "Program 5 infinity row-base formal coordinate scan",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": model.source_sha256,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": model.ambient_operation_dimension,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": len(model.tangent_basis),
        "infinity_fibre_dimension": len(infinity_indices),
        "sample_count": len(results),
        "maximum_requested_order": max_order,
        "first_obstruction_histogram": first_obstruction_histogram,
        "survivor_count": len(survivors),
        "survivors_through_requested_order": survivors,
        "samples": results,
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "The scan tests the base point and eighteen positive coordinate "
            "translates in the infinity fibre. It does not exclude mixed, "
            "negative, rational, or algebraic row-base combinations."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--max-order", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan(
            args.source,
            max_order=args.max_order,
        )
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(
            f"program5_rank_six_infinity_fibre_formal_scan: {exc}",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
