## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_third_order_lift.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test the Program 5 cubic rank-six obstruction modulo tangent freedom.

The deterministic formal lift in ``program5_rank_six_formal_arc.py`` sets the
22-dimensional homogeneous freedom in each quadratic correction equal to
zero.  Its order-three forcing has a nonzero cokernel projection.  That
deterministic residual alone does not decide whether the obstruction is
intrinsic, because every order-two correction is defined only modulo the
rank-six tangent kernel.

This script introduces all 66 free coefficients

    K_rank tensor Sym^2&lt;u,v&gt;

in the three quadratic corrections, computes their exact effect on the four
cubic forcing coefficients, and tests the resulting linear system over Q.
For the current source, the system has rank 15 and augmented rank 16.  The
script returns an exact left-null certificate showing that the chosen
first-order plane cannot be lifted through cubic parameter order.

The result decides compatibility through cubic parameter order for the chosen
two-dimensional first-order plane. It does not decide fourth order, all-order
integration, convergence, or the true operation-group quotient.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    _q,
    build_export,
)
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import (
    _flatten,
    _independent_rows_and_columns,
    _matrix_rank,
    _project_to_cokernel,
    _sympy_matrix,
)
from .program5_tangent_bridge import _extend_basis


def _sparse_vector(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    labels: Sequence&#91;str&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    return &#91;
        {"coordinate": labels&#91;index&#93;, "coefficient": _q(vector&#91;index, 0&#93;)}
        for index in range(vector.rows)
        if vector&#91;index, 0&#93; != 0
    &#93;


def _digest(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            &#91;str(sp.Rational(value)) for value in vector&#93;,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _deterministic_linear_solution(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;bool, sp.Matrix | None, int, int, int&#93;:
    rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rank()
    augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        matrix.row_join(rhs),
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()
    if rank != augmented_rank:
        return False, None, rank, augmented_rank, matrix.cols - rank
    solution_tuple = next(iter(sp.linsolve((matrix, rhs))))
    free_symbols = sorted(
        set().union(*(entry.free_symbols for entry in solution_tuple)),
        key=str,
    )
    substitution = {symbol: 0 for symbol in free_symbols}
    solution = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;sp.factor(entry.subs(substitution)) for entry in solution_tuple&#93;
    )
    if matrix * solution != rhs:
        raise AssertionError("deterministic cubic-lift solution failed")
    return True, solution, rank, augmented_rank, len(free_symbols)


def analyze_third_order(source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)
    row_kernel = _sympy_matrix(row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    rank_kernel = _sympy_matrix(rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    row_columns = &#91;row_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(row_kernel.cols)&#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;rank_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(rank_kernel.cols)&#93;
    adapted, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError("the 20+2 tangent splitting changed")
    tangent_basis = adapted
    eta0, eta1 = complement
    theta_u = eta0 + row_columns&#91;4&#93;
    theta_v = eta1 + 4 * row_columns&#91;0&#93; - 24 * row_columns&#91;1&#93; - 4 * row_columns&#91;4&#93;

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    cubic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 3)
    &#93;
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if len(operation_basis) != 115 or labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("operation basis changed")

    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for operation_row, monomial in operation_basis:
        field = sp.zeros(n, 1)
        field&#91;operation_row, 0&#93; = monomial
        bracket = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                sp.expand(value)
                for value in JQ * field - field.jacobian(V) * Q
            &#93;
        )
        coefficient_matrix = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for row, expression in enumerate(bracket):
            polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
            for column, cubic_monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
                coefficient_matrix&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(
                    cubic_monomial
                )
        variation_matrices.append(coefficient_matrix)

    def variation(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    p0 = sp.zeros(115, 1)
    p0_index = next(
        index
        for index, (row, monomial) in enumerate(operation_basis)
        if row == 3 and sp.expand(monomial - source.d**2) == 0
    )
    p0&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; = -1
    M0 = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
    for row, expression in enumerate(C):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
        for column, monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
            M0&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(monomial)
    M0 += variation(p0)

    base_rows = &#91;0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8&#93;
    zero_rows = &#91;3, 6, 7, 9, 10&#93;
    base_matrix = M0&#91;base_rows, :&#93;
    _, pivot_tuple = base_matrix.rref()
    pivot_columns = list(pivot_tuple)
    nonpivot_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(len(cubic_monomials))
        if column not in pivot_columns
    &#93;
    A0 = M0&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    B0 = M0&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;
    G0 = A0.inv()

    def blocks(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        matrix = variation(vector)
        return {
            "A": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "B": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "C": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "D": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }

    ambient_blocks = &#91;blocks(sp.eye(115)&#91;:, index&#93;) for index in range(115)&#93;
    L_columns = &#91;
        _flatten(block&#91;"D"&#93; - block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * B0)
        for block in ambient_blocks
    &#93;
    L = sp.Matrix.hstack(*L_columns)
    if _matrix_rank(L) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("tangent map rank changed")
    for vector in tangent_basis:
        if L * vector != sp.zeros(L.rows, 1):
            raise AssertionError("exported tangent basis left ker(L)")
    pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(L)
    minor = L&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns&#93;
    minor_inverse = minor.inv()

    def solve_image(forcing: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Matrix, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        residual = _project_to_cokernel(
            forcing,
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        if any(residual):
            raise AssertionError("forcing is not in the tangent image")
        coefficients = minor_inverse * forcing&#91;list(pivot_rows), :&#93;
        correction = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        for index, column in enumerate(pivot_operation_columns):
            correction&#91;column, 0&#93; = coefficients&#91;index, 0&#93;
        if L * correction != forcing:
            raise AssertionError("deterministic image solution failed")
        return correction, residual

    theta_blocks = &#91;blocks(theta_v), blocks(theta_u)&#93;
    G1 = &#91;
        -G0 * theta_blocks&#91;index&#93;&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        for index in range(2)
    &#93;

    # H2 coefficients in order v^2, u*v, u^2.
    H2 = &#91;&#93;
    for total_u in range(3):
        value = sp.zeros(len(zero_rows), len(nonpivot_columns))
        for left_u in range(2):
            right_u = total_u - left_u
            if 0 &lt;= right_u &lt; 2:
                value += (
                    theta_blocks&#91;left_u&#93;&#91;"C"&#93;
                    * G0
                    * theta_blocks&#91;right_u&#93;&#91;"B"&#93;
                )
        for c_u in range(2):
            g_u = total_u - c_u
            if 0 &lt;= g_u &lt; 2:
                value += theta_blocks&#91;c_u&#93;&#91;"C"&#93; * G1&#91;g_u&#93; * B0
        H2.append(value)

    P2_particular = &#91;&#93;
    for value in H2:
        correction, _ = solve_image(_flatten(value))
        P2_particular.append(correction)

    def inverse_order_two_blocks(
        P2_blocks: Sequence&#91;dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;&#93;,
    ) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        A2 = &#91;block&#91;"A"&#93; for block in P2_blocks&#93;
        result = &#91;&#93;
        for total_u in range(3):
            convolution = sp.zeros(6, 6)
            for left_u in range(2):
                right_u = total_u - left_u
                if 0 &lt;= right_u &lt; 2:
                    convolution += theta_blocks&#91;left_u&#93;&#91;"A"&#93; * G1&#91;right_u&#93;
            convolution += A2&#91;total_u&#93; * G0
            result.append(-G0 * convolution)
        return result

    def cubic_forcing_blocks(
        P2_blocks: Sequence&#91;dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;&#93;,
    ) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        G2 = inverse_order_two_blocks(P2_blocks)
        values = &#91;&#93;
        for total_u in range(4):
            value = sp.zeros(len(zero_rows), len(nonpivot_columns))
            # Sum C_i G_j B_k for i+j+k=3, i&gt;=1, using orders 1 and 2.
            for i_order in (1, 2):
                C_blocks = theta_blocks if i_order == 1 else P2_blocks
                C_list = &#91;block&#91;"C"&#93; for block in C_blocks&#93;
                for j_order in (0, 1, 2):
                    k_order = 3 - i_order - j_order
                    if k_order not in (0, 1, 2):
                        continue
                    G_list = &#91;G0&#93; if j_order == 0 else (G1 if j_order == 1 else G2)
                    if k_order == 0:
                        B_list = &#91;B0&#93;
                    elif k_order == 1:
                        B_list = &#91;block&#91;"B"&#93; for block in theta_blocks&#93;
                    else:
                        B_list = &#91;block&#91;"B"&#93; for block in P2_blocks&#93;
                    for c_u, C_value in enumerate(C_list):
                        for g_u, G_value in enumerate(G_list):
                            b_u = total_u - c_u - g_u
                            if 0 &lt;= b_u &lt; len(B_list):
                                value += C_value * G_value * B_list&#91;b_u&#93;
            values.append(value)
        return values

    def cubic_forcing(P2: Sequence&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        return cubic_forcing_blocks(&#91;blocks(vector) for vector in P2&#93;)

    base_H3 = cubic_forcing(P2_particular)
    base_residuals = &#91;
        _project_to_cokernel(
            _flatten(value),
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        for value in base_H3
    &#93;
    if sum(any(vector) for vector in base_residuals) != 4:
        raise AssertionError("the deterministic cubic obstruction changed")

    # Exact linear effect of a tangent-kernel addition to P2.
    tangent_blocks = &#91;blocks(vector) for vector in tangent_basis&#93;

    def bilinear_h(
        left: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        right: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        return (
            left&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * right&#91;"B"&#93;
            - left&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * right&#91;"A"&#93; * G0 * B0
            + right&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * left&#91;"B"&#93;
            - right&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * left&#91;"A"&#93; * G0 * B0
        )

    effect_v = &#91;&#93;
    effect_u = &#91;&#93;
    for tangent_block in tangent_blocks:
        effect_v.append(
            _project_to_cokernel(
                _flatten(bilinear_h(tangent_block, theta_blocks&#91;0&#93;)),
                L,
                pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
            )
        )
        effect_u.append(
            _project_to_cokernel(
                _flatten(bilinear_h(tangent_block, theta_blocks&#91;1&#93;)),
                L,
                pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
            )
        )

    # Independently replay every column of the linearized effect by adding a
    # single tangent basis vector to one quadratic coefficient and recomputing
    # the complete cubic forcing.  This guards the closed bilinear formula and
    # its u/v monomial bookkeeping.
    particular_blocks = &#91;blocks(vector) for vector in P2_particular&#93;
    direct_effect_columns_verified = 0
    for input_u in range(3):
        for tangent_index, tangent_block in enumerate(tangent_blocks):
            perturbed_blocks = &#91;
                {name: matrix.copy() for name, matrix in block.items()}
                for block in particular_blocks
            &#93;
            for name, matrix in tangent_block.items():
                perturbed_blocks&#91;input_u&#93;&#91;name&#93; += matrix
            direct_values = cubic_forcing_blocks(perturbed_blocks)
            direct_residuals = &#91;
                _project_to_cokernel(
                    _flatten(value),
                    L,
                    pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                    pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                    inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
                )
                for value in direct_values
            &#93;
            for output_u in range(4):
                expected = sp.zeros(L.rows, 1)
                if output_u == input_u:
                    expected += effect_v&#91;tangent_index&#93;
                if output_u == input_u + 1:
                    expected += effect_u&#91;tangent_index&#93;
                observed = direct_residuals&#91;output_u&#93; - base_residuals&#91;output_u&#93;
                if observed != expected:
                    raise AssertionError(
                        "direct cubic-effect replay disagrees with the "
                        f"bilinear formula in column {22 * input_u + tangent_index}"
                    )
            direct_effect_columns_verified += 1

    # Compress the 4*1400 equations to the rows that can be nonzero.
    row_keys: set&#91;tuple&#91;int, int&#93;&#93; = set()
    for output_u, residual in enumerate(base_residuals):
        row_keys.update(
            (output_u, row)
            for row, value in enumerate(residual)
            if value
        )
    for input_u in range(3):
        for effect in effect_v:
            row_keys.update(
                (input_u, row)
                for row, value in enumerate(effect)
                if value
            )
        for effect in effect_u:
            row_keys.update(
                (input_u + 1, row)
                for row, value in enumerate(effect)
                if value
            )
    ordered_rows = sorted(row_keys)
    row_index = {key: index for index, key in enumerate(ordered_rows)}
    effect_matrix = sp.zeros(len(ordered_rows), 66)
    rhs = sp.zeros(len(ordered_rows), 1)
    for output_u, residual in enumerate(base_residuals):
        for row, value in enumerate(residual):
            if value:
                rhs&#91;row_index&#91;(output_u, row)&#93;, 0&#93; = -value
    for input_u in range(3):
        for tangent_index in range(22):
            column = 22 * input_u + tangent_index
            for row, value in enumerate(effect_v&#91;tangent_index&#93;):
                if value:
                    effect_matrix&#91;row_index&#91;(input_u, row)&#93;, column&#93; += value
            for row, value in enumerate(effect_u&#91;tangent_index&#93;):
                if value:
                    effect_matrix&#91;row_index&#91;(input_u + 1, row)&#93;, column&#93; += value

    solvable, solution, effect_rank, augmented_rank, free_dimension = (
        _deterministic_linear_solution(effect_matrix, rhs)
    )
    obstruction_certificate: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None
    if not solvable:
        left_null_basis = effect_matrix.T.nullspace()
        witness = next(
            (
                vector
                for vector in left_null_basis
                if (vector.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93; != 0
            ),
            None,
        )
        if witness is None:
            raise AssertionError("rank mismatch has no left-null witness")
        first_nonzero = next(value for value in witness if value != 0)
        witness = sp.Matrix(&#91;sp.factor(value / first_nonzero) for value in witness&#93;)
        pairing = sp.factor((witness.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93;)
        if witness.T * effect_matrix != sp.zeros(1, effect_matrix.cols):
            raise AssertionError("cubic obstruction witness left the left nullspace")
        if pairing == 0:
            raise AssertionError("cubic obstruction witness does not separate the rhs")
        parameter_monomials = ("v^3", "u*v^2", "u^2*v", "u^3")
        certificate_coordinates = &#91;&#93;
        for certificate_row, coefficient in enumerate(witness):
            if coefficient == 0:
                continue
            output_u, residual_row = ordered_rows&#91;certificate_row&#93;
            schur_row, schur_column = divmod(
                residual_row,
                len(nonpivot_columns),
            )
            certificate_coordinates.append(
                {
                    "parameter_monomial": parameter_monomials&#91;output_u&#93;,
                    "schur_row_variable": str(V&#91;zero_rows&#91;schur_row&#93;&#93;),
                    "schur_column_monomial": _monomial_label(
                        cubic_monomials&#91;nonpivot_columns&#91;schur_column&#93;&#93;
                    ),
                    "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                }
            )
        obstruction_certificate = {
            "kind": "exact left-null witness",
            "equation_order": list(parameter_monomials),
            "nonzero_count": len(certificate_coordinates),
            "pairing_with_rhs": _q(pairing),
            "witness_sha256": _digest(witness),
            "coordinates": certificate_coordinates,
        }
    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "schema_version": 2,
        "name": "Program 5 cubic lift modulo quadratic tangent freedom",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": exported&#91;"summary"&#93;&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": 115,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": 22,
        "quadratic_coefficient_count": 3,
        "quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension": 66,
        "direct_effect_columns_verified": direct_effect_columns_verified,
        "compressed_cubic_equation_count": len(ordered_rows),
        "cubic_effect_rank": effect_rank,
        "cubic_augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
        "cubic_lift_solvable": solvable,
        "cubic_lift_solution_free_dimension": free_dimension if solvable else None,
        "deterministic_base_obstructed_monomials": &#91;
            "v^3",
            "u*v^2",
            "u^2*v",
            "u^3",
        &#93;,
    }
    if not solvable or solution is None:
        result&#91;"cubic_obstruction_certificate"&#93; = obstruction_certificate
        result&#91;"interpretation"&#93; = (
            "The cubic obstruction survives every tangent-kernel choice in "
            "the quadratic correction, so it is intrinsic for the chosen "
            "first-order plane."
        )
        return result

    adjusted_P2 = &#91;vector.copy() for vector in P2_particular&#93;
    tangent_adjustments = &#91;&#93;
    for input_u in range(3):
        adjustment = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        coefficients = &#91;&#93;
        for tangent_index, tangent_vector in enumerate(tangent_basis):
            coefficient = solution&#91;22 * input_u + tangent_index, 0&#93;
            if coefficient:
                adjustment += coefficient * tangent_vector
                coefficients.append(
                    {
                        "tangent_basis_index": tangent_index,
                        "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                    }
                )
        adjusted_P2&#91;input_u&#93; += adjustment
        tangent_adjustments.append(
            {
                "monomial": ("v^2", "u*v", "u^2")&#91;input_u&#93;,
                "coefficient_count": len(coefficients),
                "coefficients": coefficients,
                "adjustment_nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(adjustment, labels)),
                "adjustment_sha256": _digest(adjustment),
            }
        )

    adjusted_H3 = cubic_forcing(adjusted_P2)
    adjusted_residuals = &#91;
        _project_to_cokernel(
            _flatten(value),
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        for value in adjusted_H3
    &#93;
    if any(any(vector) for vector in adjusted_residuals):
        raise AssertionError("the solved tangent adjustment did not kill the cubic obstruction")
    P3 = &#91;&#93;
    for value in adjusted_H3:
        correction, _ = solve_image(_flatten(value))
        P3.append(correction)

    result.update(
        {
            "quadratic_tangent_adjustments": tangent_adjustments,
            "adjusted_quadratic_corrections": &#91;
                {
                    "monomial": ("v^2", "u*v", "u^2")&#91;index&#93;,
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(vector, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(vector),
                    "vector": _sparse_vector(vector, labels),
                }
                for index, vector in enumerate(adjusted_P2)
            &#93;,
            "cubic_obstruction_after_adjustment": 0,
            "cubic_corrections": &#91;
                {
                    "monomial": (
                        "v^3",
                        "u*v^2",
                        "u^2*v",
                        "u^3",
                    )&#91;index&#93;,
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(vector, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(vector),
                    "vector": _sparse_vector(vector, labels),
                }
                for index, vector in enumerate(P3)
            &#93;,
            "interpretation": (
                "The deterministic cubic obstruction is removable by the "
                "22-dimensional tangent freedom in the quadratic correction. "
                "The chosen two-dimensional branch therefore survives through "
                "cubic parameter order."
            ),
        }
    )
    return result


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_third_order(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_rank_six_third_order_lift: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_row_killing.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Adapt a Program 5 row-killing packet to the generic operation complex.

The expected packet exports the exact linear operator together with candidate
row-killers, proved source and target automorphism images, stable-presentation
changes, and optional obstruction functionals.  Stable-presentation changes
are represented as recharts rather than fixed-presentation gauge.

This adapter is an interface, not a Program 5 theorem: it cannot decide which
row-killing directions are genuine automorphisms or discover missing stable
presentation changes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from ..core import ContractError, analyze_document


def _vectors(packet: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;, key: str) -&gt; list&#91;Any&#93;:
    values = packet.get(key, &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(values, list):
        raise ContractError(f"Program 5 field {key!r} must be a list")
    return values


def adapt_document(packet: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    if packet.get("schema_version") != 1:
        raise ContractError("Program 5 adapter schema_version must equal 1")
    operator = packet.get("operator")
    if not isinstance(operator, list):
        raise ContractError("Program 5 packet requires operator rows")
    deformation_dimension = packet.get("deformation_dimension")
    if not isinstance(deformation_dimension, int):
        deformation_dimension = len(operator&#91;0&#93;) if operator else None
    equation_dimension = packet.get("equation_dimension")
    if not isinstance(equation_dimension, int):
        equation_dimension = len(operator)
    if not isinstance(deformation_dimension, int):
        raise ContractError("deformation_dimension is required for an empty operator")

    candidates = _vectors(packet, "candidate_row_killers")
    source = _vectors(packet, "source_automorphisms")
    target = _vectors(packet, "target_automorphisms")
    stable = _vectors(packet, "stable_presentation_changes")
    extra_gauge = packet.get("extra_gauge_spaces", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(extra_gauge, list):
        raise ContractError("extra_gauge_spaces must be a list")

    actions: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;
        {
            "name": "row_killing_candidates",
            "role": "formal",
            "generators": candidates,
        },
        {
            "name": "source_automorphisms",
            "role": "source_automorphism",
            "parent": "row_killing_candidates" if candidates else None,
            "generators": source,
        },
        {
            "name": "target_automorphisms",
            "role": "target_automorphism",
            "parent": "row_killing_candidates" if candidates else None,
            "generators": target,
        },
    &#93;
    gauge_actions = &#91;"source_automorphisms", "target_automorphisms"&#93;
    for index, space in enumerate(extra_gauge):
        if not isinstance(space, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"extra_gauge_spaces&#91;{index}&#93; must be an object")
        name = str(space.get("name", f"extra-gauge-{index}"))
        actions.append(
            {
                "name": name,
                "role": str(space.get("role", "gauge")),
                "parent": space.get("parent"),
                "generators": space.get("generators", &#91;&#93;),
            }
        )
        gauge_actions.append(name)

    layer: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "id": str(packet.get("layer_id", "program5-row-killing")),
        "deformation_dimension": deformation_dimension,
        "equation_dimension": equation_dimension,
        "deformation_basis": packet.get("deformation_basis"),
        "equation_basis": packet.get("equation_basis"),
        "operator": operator,
        "actions": actions,
        "gauge_actions": gauge_actions,
        "recharts": &#91;
            {
                "name": "stable_presentation_changes",
                "generators": stable,
            }
        &#93;,
        "obstruction_functionals": packet.get("obstruction_functionals", &#91;&#93;),
        "metadata": {
            "adapter": "program5_row_killing",
            "scope": packet.get(
                "scope",
                "Exact tangent-space quotient only; nonlinear coupling is not audited.",
            ),
        },
    }
    if "forcing" in packet:
        layer&#91;"forcing"&#93; = packet&#91;"forcing"&#93;
    if layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93; is None:
        layer.pop("deformation_basis")
    if layer&#91;"equation_basis"&#93; is None:
        layer.pop("equation_basis")

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": str(packet.get("name", "Program 5 row-killing quotient")),
        "field": packet.get("field", {"kind": "rational"}),
        "report_options": packet.get("report_options", {}),
        "layers": &#91;layer&#93;,
        "transitions": packet.get("transitions", &#91;&#93;),
    }


def analyze_packet(packet: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    return analyze_document(adapt_document(packet))


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("input", type=Path, help="Program 5 row-killing packet")
    parser.add_argument("-o", "--output", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--emit-contract", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        packet = json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if not isinstance(packet, Mapping):
            raise ContractError("top-level Program 5 packet must be an object")
        contract = adapt_document(packet)
        report = analyze_document(contract)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_row_killing: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if args.emit_contract:
        args.emit_contract.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.emit_contract.write_text(
            json.dumps(contract, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    rendered = json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_tangent_bridge.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Analyze the two rank-six tangent directions outside the row-zero chart.

The exact public Program 5 compression packet gives two nested tangent spaces
inside the 115-dimensional space of weight-preserving quadratic vector fields:

    K_row  = directions preserving the equations that the a,d,q,h,k rows vanish;
    K_rank = tangent directions to the rank-at-most-six cubic-coordinate locus
             at P0=-d^2 e_a.

Their dimensions are 20 and 22.  This script verifies ``K_row &lt;= K_rank``,
constructs an adapted basis ``K_row + &lt;eta_0,eta_1&gt;``, and restricts the exact
quartic functional Lambda_4(O_4(P)) to

    P = P0 + sum_i s_i K_row&#91;i&#93; + u eta_0 + v eta_1.

The calculation is exact over Q.  It is a tangent-coupling diagnostic, not an
integration theorem for the rank-six determinantal locus and not a quotient by
all polynomial/stable automorphisms.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _basis_index,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    _q,
    build_export,
)


def _sympy_matrix(values: Sequence&#91;Sequence&#91;Any&#93;&#93;) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
    return sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            &#91;sp.Rational(value) for value in row&#93;
            for row in values
        &#93;
    )


def _extend_basis(
    base_columns: Sequence&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;,
    candidates: Sequence&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;, list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;&#93;:
    accepted = list(base_columns)
    rank = sp.Matrix.hstack(*accepted).rank() if accepted else 0
    added: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for candidate in candidates:
        trial = sp.Matrix.hstack(*accepted, candidate)
        trial_rank = trial.rank()
        if trial_rank &gt; rank:
            accepted.append(candidate)
            added.append(candidate)
            rank = trial_rank
    return accepted, added


def _sparse_vector(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    labels: Sequence&#91;str&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    return &#91;
        {"coordinate": labels&#91;index&#93;, "coefficient": _q(vector&#91;index, 0&#93;)}
        for index in range(vector.rows)
        if vector&#91;index, 0&#93; != 0
    &#93;


def _polynomial_terms(
    expression: sp.Expr,
    variables: Sequence&#91;sp.Symbol&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    polynomial = sp.Poly(sp.expand(expression), *variables, domain=sp.QQ)
    terms = &#91;&#93;
    for exponents, coefficient in polynomial.terms():
        monomial_parts = &#91;&#93;
        for variable, exponent in zip(variables, exponents):
            if exponent == 0:
                continue
            monomial_parts.append(
                str(variable) if exponent == 1 else f"{variable}^{exponent}"
            )
        terms.append(
            {
                "exponents": list(exponents),
                "monomial": "*".join(monomial_parts) or "1",
                "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
            }
        )
    return terms


def analyze_bridge(source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)

    row_kernel_matrix = _sympy_matrix(
        row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;
    )
    rank_kernel_matrix = _sympy_matrix(
        rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;
    )
    row_columns = &#91;
        row_kernel_matrix&#91;:, index&#93;
        for index in range(row_kernel_matrix.cols)
    &#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;
        rank_kernel_matrix&#91;:, index&#93;
        for index in range(rank_kernel_matrix.cols)
    &#93;
    if row_kernel_matrix.rank() != 20 or rank_kernel_matrix.rank() != 22:
        raise AssertionError("the exported tangent bases lost rank")
    if sp.Matrix.hstack(rank_kernel_matrix, row_kernel_matrix).rank() != 22:
        raise AssertionError("the row-zero tangent is not contained in the rank-six tangent")

    adapted_columns, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted_columns) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError(
            f"expected a two-dimensional tangent excess, found {len(complement)}"
        )
    adapted_matrix = sp.Matrix.hstack(*adapted_columns)

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    if len(operation_basis) != 115:
        raise AssertionError("the operation basis no longer has dimension 115")
    reconstructed_labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if reconstructed_labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("the operation-basis order changed")

    parameters = sp.symbols("s0:20") + sp.symbols("u v")
    coefficient_vector = adapted_matrix * sp.Matrix(parameters)
    p0_index = _basis_index(operation_basis, 3, source.d**2)
    coefficient_vector&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; -= 1

    P = sp.zeros(n, 1)
    for coefficient, (row, monomial) in zip(coefficient_vector, operation_basis):
        if coefficient:
            P&#91;row, 0&#93; += coefficient * monomial
    P = sp.Matrix(&#91;sp.expand(value) for value in P&#93;)
    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    O4 = (
        C.jacobian(V) * P
        - P.jacobian(V) * C
        + sp.Rational(1, 2) * source.D2_11(Q, P, P)
        - P.jacobian(V) * (JQ * P - P.jacobian(V) * Q)
        - sp.Rational(1, 2) * source.D2_11(P, Q, Q)
    )
    Lambda = sp.expand(
        sum(
            coefficient
            * sp.Poly(sp.expand(O4&#91;row&#93;), *V).coeff_monomial(monomial)
            for row, monomial, coefficient in source.terms
        )
    )
    polynomial = sp.Poly(Lambda, *parameters, domain=sp.QQ)
    if polynomial.total_degree() &gt; 2:
        raise AssertionError("the quartic functional restriction should be quadratic")

    row_parameters = parameters&#91;:20&#93;
    extra_parameters = parameters&#91;20:&#93;
    row_chart_restriction = sp.expand(
        Lambda.subs({parameter: 0 for parameter in extra_parameters})
    )
    if row_chart_restriction != 1:
        raise AssertionError(
            f"Lambda_4 is not constant one on the row-zero slice: {row_chart_restriction}"
        )

    extra_plane_restriction = sp.factor(
        Lambda.subs({parameter: 0 for parameter in row_parameters})
    )
    linear_extra = &#91;
        sp.expand(sp.diff(Lambda, parameter).subs({value: 0 for value in parameters}))
        for parameter in extra_parameters
    &#93;
    hessian_extra = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            &#91;
                sp.expand(
                    sp.diff(Lambda, left, right).subs(
                        {value: 0 for value in parameters}
                    )
                )
                for right in extra_parameters
            &#93;
            for left in extra_parameters
        &#93;
    )

    nonconstant = sp.expand(Lambda - 1) != 0
    pure_row_terms = &#91;&#93;
    mixed_terms = &#91;&#93;
    pure_extra_terms = &#91;&#93;
    for term in _polynomial_terms(Lambda - 1, parameters):
        exponents = term&#91;"exponents"&#93;
        row_degree = sum(exponents&#91;:20&#93;)
        extra_degree = sum(exponents&#91;20:&#93;)
        if row_degree and not extra_degree:
            pure_row_terms.append(term)
        elif row_degree and extra_degree:
            mixed_terms.append(term)
        elif extra_degree:
            pure_extra_terms.append(term)
    if pure_row_terms:
        raise AssertionError("pure row-zero tangent terms should vanish")

    rational_cancellation: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None
    u, v = extra_parameters
    if linear_extra&#91;0&#93; != 0:
        value = sp.factor(-1 / linear_extra&#91;0&#93;)
        if sp.expand(extra_plane_restriction.subs({u: value, v: 0})) == 0:
            rational_cancellation = {
                "u": _q(value),
                "v": 0,
                "verification": "Lambda_4=0 on the complement plane",
            }
    if rational_cancellation is None and linear_extra&#91;1&#93; != 0:
        value = sp.factor(-1 / linear_extra&#91;1&#93;)
        if sp.expand(extra_plane_restriction.subs({u: 0, v: value})) == 0:
            rational_cancellation = {
                "u": 0,
                "v": _q(value),
                "verification": "Lambda_4=0 on the complement plane",
            }

    summary = exported&#91;"summary"&#93;
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": "Program 5 row-zero to rank-six tangent bridge",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": summary&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": 115,
        "row_zero_tangent_dimension": 20,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": 22,
        "inclusion_rank": 20,
        "tangent_excess_dimension": 2,
        "adapted_basis": {
            "first_20": "the exported row-zero tangent basis",
            "complement": &#91;
                {
                    "name": f"eta_{index}",
                    "vector": _sparse_vector(vector, operation_labels),
                }
                for index, vector in enumerate(complement)
            &#93;,
        },
        "quartic_restriction": {
            "functional": "Lambda_4(O_4(P))",
            "total_degree": polynomial.total_degree(),
            "term_count": len(polynomial.terms()),
            "constant_on_row_zero_slice": True,
            "row_zero_value": 1,
            "nonconstant_on_full_rank_six_tangent_plane": nonconstant,
            "extra_plane_expression": str(extra_plane_restriction),
            "linear_coefficients_in_eta_coordinates": &#91;
                _q(value) for value in linear_extra
            &#93;,
            "extra_hessian": &#91;
                &#91;_q(hessian_extra&#91;row, column&#93;) for column in range(2)&#93;
                for row in range(2)
            &#93;,
            "pure_extra_terms": pure_extra_terms,
            "mixed_row_extra_terms": mixed_terms,
            "full_terms": _polynomial_terms(Lambda, parameters),
            "rational_cancellation_on_complement_plane": rational_cancellation,
        },
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "The two complement vectors are tangent to the rank-at-most-six "
            "determinantal locus. This calculation does not prove that their "
            "finite affine plane integrates inside that locus, nor that they "
            "survive quotienting by all source, target, and stable operations."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_bridge(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_tangent_bridge: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

<a id="source-2a4ffcbda460e83d"></a>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program6_legacy.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Adapt the original Program 6 chart-correspondence JSON contract.

The legacy contract has one operator together with ``gauge_vectors`` and
``rechart_vectors`` per layer.  This adapter interprets the supplied gauge
vectors as the declared filtered fixed-chart operation space and the supplied
rechart vectors as adjacent-presentation directions.  It does not infer the
formal, polynomial, or approximate-root subgroup hierarchy.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from ..core import ContractError, analyze_document


def transport_support(k: int, monomials: Sequence&#91;Sequence&#91;int&#93;&#93;) -&gt; list&#91;list&#91;int&#93;&#93;:
    transported: set&#91;tuple&#91;int, int&#93;&#93; = set()
    for monomial in monomials:
        if len(monomial) != 2:
            raise ContractError(f"invalid Program 6 monomial {monomial!r}")
        i, j = monomial
        if not isinstance(i, int) or not isinstance(j, int) or j &lt; 0:
            raise ContractError("Program 6 support exponents must be integers with j&gt;=0")
        for t in range(j + 1):
            transported.add((i - k * t, j - t))
    return &#91;
        &#91;i, j&#93;
        for i, j in sorted(transported, key=lambda pair: (-pair&#91;1&#93;, pair&#91;0&#93;))
    &#93;


def adapt_document(legacy: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;) -&gt; tuple&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;, dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    if legacy.get("schema_version") != 1:
        raise ContractError("legacy Program 6 schema_version must equal 1")
    raw_layers = legacy.get("layers")
    if not isinstance(raw_layers, list) or not raw_layers:
        raise ContractError("legacy Program 6 contract has no layers")

    layers: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    support_closures: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for index, raw in enumerate(raw_layers):
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"legacy layer {index} is not an object")
        identifier = str(raw.get("label", raw.get("r", f"layer-{index}")))
        operator = raw.get("operator")
        if not isinstance(operator, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: operator must be a list")
        deformation_dimension = len(operator&#91;0&#93;) if operator else raw.get("ambient_dimension")
        if not isinstance(deformation_dimension, int):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}: ambient_dimension is required for an empty operator"
            )
        gauge_vectors = raw.get("gauge_vectors", &#91;&#93;)
        rechart_vectors = raw.get("rechart_vectors", &#91;&#93;)
        if not isinstance(gauge_vectors, list) or not isinstance(rechart_vectors, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: legacy vectors must be lists")
        layer = {
            "id": identifier,
            "deformation_dimension": deformation_dimension,
            "equation_dimension": len(operator),
            "operator": operator,
            "actions": &#91;
                {
                    "name": "filtered_fixed_chart",
                    "role": "filtered",
                    "generators": gauge_vectors,
                }
            &#93;,
            "gauge_actions": &#91;"filtered_fixed_chart"&#93;,
            "recharts": &#91;
                {
                    "name": "adjacent_chart_tangents",
                    "generators": rechart_vectors,
                }
            &#93;,
            "metadata": {
                "legacy_label": identifier,
                "legacy_contract": True,
            },
        }
        layers.append(layer)

        support = raw.get("support_transport")
        if support is not None:
            if not isinstance(support, Mapping):
                raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: support_transport must be an object")
            k = support.get("k")
            monomials = support.get("monomials")
            if not isinstance(k, int) or not isinstance(monomials, list):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{identifier}: support_transport requires integer k and monomials"
                )
            support_closures.append(
                {
                    "layer": identifier,
                    "k": k,
                    "input": monomials,
                    "closure": transport_support(k, monomials),
                }
            )

    contract = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": f"Program 6 adapter: {legacy.get('name', 'legacy chart contract')}",
        "field": {"kind": "rational"},
        "layers": layers,
        "transitions": &#91;&#93;,
    }
    adapter_metadata = {
        "adapter": "program6_legacy",
        "limitations": &#91;
            "The legacy gauge vectors are treated as the declared filtered subgroup.",
            "The adapter does not discover adjacent charts or the approximate-root subgroup.",
            *&#91;str(value) for value in legacy.get("limitations", &#91;&#93;)&#93;,
        &#93;,
        "support_closures": support_closures,
    }
    return contract, adapter_metadata


def analyze_legacy_document(legacy: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, metadata = adapt_document(legacy)
    report = analyze_document(contract)
    report&#91;"adapter_metadata"&#93; = metadata
    return report


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("input", type=Path, help="legacy Program 6 JSON contract")
    parser.add_argument("-o", "--output", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--emit-contract",
        type=Path,
        help="also write the converted generic contract",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        legacy = json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if not isinstance(legacy, Mapping):
            raise ContractError("top-level legacy value must be an object")
        contract, metadata = adapt_document(legacy)
        report = analyze_document(contract)
        report&#91;"adapter_metadata"&#93; = metadata
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f"program6_legacy: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if args.emit_contract:
        args.emit_contract.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.emit_contract.write_text(
            json.dumps(contract, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
    rendered = json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/core.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""CLI and document-level orchestration for filtered operation complexes."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .fields import (
    ContractError,
    NumberField,
    NumberFieldElement,  # noqa: F401 - re-exported by package __init__
    RationalField,  # noqa: F401 - re-exported by package __init__
    build_field,
)
from .layer import LayerAudit, parse_layer
from .linear import dot, serialize_vector, solve_affine
from .transition import audit_transition


def _audit_affine_forcing(
    audit: LayerAudit,
    *,
    field: Any,
    include_vectors: bool,
) -&gt; None:
    """Augment one layer report with the exact equation ``D*x=-Phi``."""

    data = audit.data
    if data.forcing is None:
        return

    rhs = &#91;-value for value in data.forcing&#93;
    solvable, particular = solve_affine(
        data.operator,
        rhs,
        rows=data.equation_dimension,
        columns=data.deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    complete_pairings = &#91;
        dot(functional, data.forcing, field)
        for functional in data.left_kernel_basis
    &#93;
    pairing_compatible = all(not value for value in complete_pairings)
    if pairing_compatible != solvable:
        raise ContractError(
            f"{data.identifier}: affine solvability disagrees with the "
            "complete left-null pairing test"
        )

    audit.result.update(
        {
            "affine_equation": "D*x=-forcing",
            "forcing_solvable": solvable,
            "forcing_compatibility_verified": True,
            "complete_left_null_forcing_pairings": &#91;
                field.serialize(value) for value in complete_pairings
            &#93;,
            "affine_solution_dimension": (
                len(data.kernel_basis) if solvable else None
            ),
        }
    )
    if include_vectors and particular is not None:
        audit.result&#91;"particular_solution"&#93; = serialize_vector(
            field,
            particular,
        )


def analyze_document(document: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    if document.get("schema_version") != 1:
        raise ContractError("schema_version must equal 1")
    field = build_field(document.get("field"))
    report_options = document.get("report_options", {})
    if not isinstance(report_options, Mapping):
        raise ContractError("report_options must be an object")
    include_vectors = bool(report_options.get("include_vectors", False))

    raw_layers = document.get("layers")
    if not isinstance(raw_layers, list) or not raw_layers:
        raise ContractError("layers must be a nonempty list")
    audits = &#91;
        parse_layer(field, raw, include_vectors=include_vectors)
        for raw in raw_layers
    &#93;
    for audit in audits:
        _audit_affine_forcing(
            audit,
            field=field,
            include_vectors=include_vectors,
        )

    by_identifier: dict&#91;str, LayerAudit&#93; = {}
    for audit in audits:
        if audit.data.identifier in by_identifier:
            raise ContractError(
                f"duplicate layer id {audit.data.identifier!r}"
            )
        by_identifier&#91;audit.data.identifier&#93; = audit

    raw_transitions = document.get("transitions", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_transitions, list):
        raise ContractError("transitions must be a list")
    transitions = &#91;
        audit_transition(field, raw, by_identifier)
        for raw in raw_transitions
    &#93;

    forced_layers = &#91;
        audit for audit in audits if audit.data.forcing is not None
    &#93;
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": str(document.get("name", "filtered operation complex")),
        "field": {
            "kind": field.kind,
            **(
                {
                    "modulus": &#91;str(value) for value in field.modulus&#93;,
                    "symbol": field.symbol,
                }
                if isinstance(field, NumberField)
                else {}
            ),
        },
        "layers": &#91;audit.result for audit in audits&#93;,
        "transitions": transitions,
        "all_true_quotients_zero": all(
            audit.result&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93; == 0
            for audit in audits
        ),
        "all_transitions_verified": all(
            transition&#91;"chain_map_verified"&#93; for transition in transitions
        ),
        "all_forcing_equations_solvable": (
            all(audit.result&#91;"forcing_solvable"&#93; for audit in forced_layers)
            if forced_layers
            else None
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "input",
        type=Path,
        help="filtered-operation JSON contract",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "-o",
        "--output",
        type=Path,
        help="write report as JSON",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        document = json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if not isinstance(document, Mapping):
            raise ContractError("top-level JSON value must be an object")
        report = analyze_document(document)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        print(f"filtered_operation_complex: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/fields.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Exact coefficient fields used by filtered-operation audits."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence


class ContractError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a filtered-operation contract is internally inconsistent."""


def _q(value: Any) -&gt; Fraction:
    if isinstance(value, Fraction):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise TypeError("booleans are not exact scalar coefficients")
    if isinstance(value, int):
        return Fraction(value)
    if isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            return Fraction(value)
        except (ValueError, ZeroDivisionError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid rational coefficient {value!r}") from exc
    raise TypeError(
        f"coefficient {value!r} has type {type(value).__name__}; "
        "use integers or rational strings"
    )


class ExactField:
    kind: str

    @property
    def zero(self) -&gt; Any:
        raise NotImplementedError

    @property
    def one(self) -&gt; Any:
        raise NotImplementedError

    def parse(self, value: Any) -&gt; Any:
        raise NotImplementedError

    def serialize(self, value: Any) -&gt; Any:
        raise NotImplementedError


class RationalField(ExactField):
    kind = "rational"

    @property
    def zero(self) -&gt; Fraction:
        return Fraction(0)

    @property
    def one(self) -&gt; Fraction:
        return Fraction(1)

    def parse(self, value: Any) -&gt; Fraction:
        return _q(value)

    def serialize(self, value: Fraction) -&gt; str:
        value = _q(value)
        return str(value.numerator) if value.denominator == 1 else str(value)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class NumberFieldElement:
    field: "NumberField"
    coefficients: tuple&#91;Fraction, ...&#93;

    def _coerce(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self.field.parse(other)

    def __bool__(self) -&gt; bool:
        return any(self.coefficients)

    def __add__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        other = self._coerce(other)
        return self.field.element(
            &#91;left + right for left, right in zip(self.coefficients, other.coefficients)&#93;
        )

    __radd__ = __add__

    def __neg__(self) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self.field.element(&#91;-value for value in self.coefficients&#93;)

    def __sub__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self + (-self._coerce(other))

    def __rsub__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self._coerce(other) - self

    def __mul__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        other = self._coerce(other)
        degree = self.field.degree
        temporary = &#91;Fraction(0) for _ in range(2 * degree - 1)&#93;
        for i, left in enumerate(self.coefficients):
            for j, right in enumerate(other.coefficients):
                temporary&#91;i + j&#93; += left * right
        modulus = self.field.modulus
        leading_modulus = modulus&#91;degree&#93;
        for power in range(2 * degree - 2, degree - 1, -1):
            leading = temporary&#91;power&#93;
            if not leading:
                continue
            temporary&#91;power&#93; = Fraction(0)
            for index in range(degree):
                temporary&#91;power - degree + index&#93; -= (
                    leading * modulus&#91;index&#93; / leading_modulus
                )
        return self.field.element(temporary&#91;:degree&#93;)

    __rmul__ = __mul__

    def inverse(self) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        if not self:
            raise ZeroDivisionError("zero has no inverse")
        field = self.field
        basis = &#91;
            field.element(&#91;field.one_rational if i == j else 0 for i in range(field.degree)&#93;)
            for j in range(field.degree)
        &#93;
        columns = &#91;(self * basis_vector).coefficients for basis_vector in basis&#93;
        matrix: list&#91;list&#91;Fraction&#93;&#93; = &#91;
            &#91;columns&#91;column&#93;&#91;row&#93; for column in range(field.degree)&#93;
            + &#91;Fraction(1 if row == 0 else 0)&#93;
            for row in range(field.degree)
        &#93;
        for column in range(field.degree):
            pivot = next(
                (row for row in range(column, field.degree) if matrix&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93;),
                None,
            )
            if pivot is None:
                raise ZeroDivisionError("field element is not invertible modulo the modulus")
            matrix&#91;column&#93;, matrix&#91;pivot&#93; = matrix&#91;pivot&#93;, matrix&#91;column&#93;
            scale = matrix&#91;column&#93;&#91;column&#93;
            matrix&#91;column&#93; = &#91;entry / scale for entry in matrix&#91;column&#93;&#93;
            for row in range(field.degree):
                if row == column:
                    continue
                factor = matrix&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93;
                if factor:
                    matrix&#91;row&#93; = &#91;
                        left - factor * right
                        for left, right in zip(matrix&#91;row&#93;, matrix&#91;column&#93;)
                    &#93;
        return field.element(&#91;matrix&#91;row&#93;&#91;-1&#93; for row in range(field.degree)&#93;)

    def __truediv__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self * self._coerce(other).inverse()

    def __rtruediv__(self, other: Any) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        return self._coerce(other) * self.inverse()

    def __pow__(self, exponent: int) -&gt; "NumberFieldElement":
        if exponent &lt; 0:
            return self.inverse() ** (-exponent)
        result = self.field.one
        base = self
        remaining = exponent
        while remaining:
            if remaining &amp; 1:
                result = result * base
            base = base * base
            remaining //= 2
        return result


class NumberField(ExactField):
    kind = "number_field"
    one_rational = Fraction(1)

    def __init__(self, modulus: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;, symbol: str = "u") -&gt; None:
        if len(modulus) &lt; 2:
            raise ContractError("number-field modulus must have positive degree")
        self.modulus = tuple(_q(value) for value in modulus)
        self.degree = len(self.modulus) - 1
        if self.modulus&#91;-1&#93; == 0:
            raise ContractError("number-field modulus has zero leading coefficient")
        self.symbol = symbol
        self._zero = NumberFieldElement(self, (Fraction(0),) * self.degree)
        self._one = self.element(&#91;1&#93;)

    @property
    def zero(self) -&gt; NumberFieldElement:
        return self._zero

    @property
    def one(self) -&gt; NumberFieldElement:
        return self._one

    def element(self, coefficients: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;) -&gt; NumberFieldElement:
        parsed = &#91;_q(value) for value in coefficients&#93;
        if len(parsed) &gt; self.degree:
            raise ContractError(
                f"number-field element has {len(parsed)} coefficients; "
                f"expected at most {self.degree}"
            )
        parsed.extend(Fraction(0) for _ in range(self.degree - len(parsed)))
        return NumberFieldElement(self, tuple(parsed))

    def parse(self, value: Any) -&gt; NumberFieldElement:
        if isinstance(value, NumberFieldElement):
            if value.field.modulus != self.modulus:
                raise ContractError("number-field coefficient belongs to another field")
            return value
        if isinstance(value, (int, str, Fraction)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
            return self.element(&#91;value&#93;)
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            value = value.get("coefficients")
        if isinstance(value, Sequence) and not isinstance(value, (str, bytes)):
            return self.element(value)
        raise TypeError(f"cannot parse number-field coefficient {value!r}")

    def serialize(self, value: NumberFieldElement) -&gt; list&#91;str&#93;:
        value = self.parse(value)
        return &#91;
            str(coefficient.numerator)
            if coefficient.denominator == 1
            else str(coefficient)
            for coefficient in value.coefficients
        &#93;


def build_field(specification: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93; | None) -&gt; ExactField:
    specification = specification or {"kind": "rational"}
    kind = specification.get("kind", "rational")
    if kind == "rational":
        return RationalField()
    if kind == "number_field":
        modulus = specification.get("modulus")
        if not isinstance(modulus, list):
            raise ContractError("number_field requires a low-to-high modulus list")
        return NumberField(modulus, str(specification.get("symbol", "u")))
    raise ContractError(f"unsupported coefficient field kind {kind!r}")
</code></pre>
