## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/layer.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Layer parsing and quotient computation."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .fields import ContractError, ExactField
from .linear import (
    Matrix,
    Vector,
    dot,
    independent_extension,
    left_nullspace,
    matrix_vector_product,
    nullspace,
    parse_generators,
    parse_matrix,
    parse_vector,
    row_matrix_product,
    serialize_vector,
    vector_in_span,
    vectors_to_column_matrix,
)


@dataclass
class ActionSpace:
    name: str
    role: str
    parent: str | None
    labels: list&#91;str&#93;
    generators: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    source_dimension: int
    source_basis: list&#91;str&#93;
    action_matrix: Matrix
    source_stabilizer_basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    input_form: str


@dataclass
class RechartSpace:
    name: str
    labels: list&#91;str&#93;
    generators: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;


@dataclass
class LayerData:
    identifier: str
    deformation_dimension: int
    equation_dimension: int
    operator: Matrix
    kernel_basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    left_kernel_basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    actions: dict&#91;str, ActionSpace&#93;
    recharts: list&#91;RechartSpace&#93;
    gauge_actions: list&#91;str&#93;
    gauge_basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    explained_basis: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    quotient_representatives: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    forcing: Vector | None
    obstruction_functionals: list&#91;Vector&#93;
    deformation_basis: list&#91;str&#93;
    equation_basis: list&#91;str&#93;
    metadata: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;


@dataclass
class LayerAudit:
    data: LayerData
    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93;


def _serialize_matrix(field: ExactField, matrix: Matrix) -&gt; list&#91;list&#91;Any&#93;&#93;:
    return &#91;serialize_vector(field, row) for row in matrix&#93;


def _basis_from_generators(
    generators: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    *,
    ambient_dimension: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; list&#91;Vector&#93;:
    basis, _ = independent_extension(
        &#91;&#93;,
        generators,
        ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    return basis


def _columns(matrix: Matrix, *, rows: int, columns: int) -&gt; list&#91;Vector&#93;:
    return &#91;
        &#91;matrix&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93; for row in range(rows)&#93;
        for column in range(columns)
    &#93;


def _parse_source_basis(
    raw: Any,
    *,
    dimension: int,
    default: Sequence&#91;str&#93;,
    name: str,
) -&gt; list&#91;str&#93;:
    if raw is None:
        return list(default)
    if not isinstance(raw, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: source_basis must be a list")
    basis = &#91;str(value) for value in raw&#93;
    if len(basis) != dimension:
        raise ContractError(
            f"{name}: source_basis has length {len(basis)}, expected {dimension}"
        )
    return basis


def _parse_action(
    field: ExactField,
    action: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;,
    *,
    identifier: str,
    index: int,
    deformation_dimension: int,
    operator: Matrix,
) -&gt; ActionSpace:
    name = str(action.get("name", f"action-{index}"))
    role = str(action.get("role", "operation"))
    parent = action.get("parent")
    if parent is not None:
        parent = str(parent)

    has_generators = "generators" in action
    has_matrix = "action_matrix" in action
    if has_generators == has_matrix:
        raise ContractError(
            f"{identifier}.{name}: supply exactly one of generators or action_matrix"
        )

    if has_generators:
        values = action.get("generators")
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}.{name}: generators must be a list")
        labels, generators = parse_generators(
            field,
            values,
            width=deformation_dimension,
            name=f"{identifier}.{name}",
        )
        source_dimension = len(generators)
        declared_dimension = action.get("source_dimension")
        if declared_dimension is not None and declared_dimension != source_dimension:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}.{name}: source_dimension does not match generators"
            )
        source_basis = _parse_source_basis(
            action.get("source_basis"),
            dimension=source_dimension,
            default=labels,
            name=f"{identifier}.{name}",
        )
        action_matrix = vectors_to_column_matrix(
            generators,
            ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        input_form = "generators"
    else:
        values = action.get("action_matrix")
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}.{name}: action_matrix must be a list")
        source_dimension = action.get("source_dimension")
        if not isinstance(source_dimension, int):
            if deformation_dimension:
                if len(values) != deformation_dimension or not values:
                    raise ContractError(
                        f"{identifier}.{name}: cannot infer source_dimension"
                    )
                first_row = values&#91;0&#93;
                if not isinstance(first_row, list):
                    raise ContractError(
                        f"{identifier}.{name}: action_matrix rows must be lists"
                    )
                source_dimension = len(first_row)
            else:
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{identifier}.{name}: source_dimension is required "
                    "for an action into the zero deformation space"
                )
        if source_dimension &lt; 0:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}.{name}: source_dimension must be nonnegative"
            )
        source_basis = _parse_source_basis(
            action.get("source_basis"),
            dimension=source_dimension,
            default=&#91;f"u{column}" for column in range(source_dimension)&#93;,
            name=f"{identifier}.{name}",
        )
        action_matrix = parse_matrix(
            field,
            values,
            rows=deformation_dimension,
            columns=source_dimension,
            name=f"{identifier}.{name}.action_matrix",
        )
        generators = _columns(
            action_matrix,
            rows=deformation_dimension,
            columns=source_dimension,
        )
        labels = list(source_basis)
        input_form = "action_matrix"

    for generator_index, generator in enumerate(generators):
        if any(matrix_vector_product(operator, generator, field)):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}.{name}: source basis vector {generator_index} "
                "does not map into ker(D)"
            )

    basis = _basis_from_generators(
        generators,
        ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    source_stabilizer_basis = nullspace(
        action_matrix,
        rows=deformation_dimension,
        columns=source_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    if len(basis) + len(source_stabilizer_basis) != source_dimension:
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}.{name}: action rank-nullity failed")

    return ActionSpace(
        name=name,
        role=role,
        parent=parent,
        labels=labels,
        generators=generators,
        basis=basis,
        source_dimension=source_dimension,
        source_basis=source_basis,
        action_matrix=action_matrix,
        source_stabilizer_basis=source_stabilizer_basis,
        input_form=input_form,
    )


def parse_layer(
    field: ExactField,
    raw: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;,
    *,
    include_vectors: bool,
) -&gt; LayerAudit:
    identifier = str(raw.get("id", raw.get("label", "unnamed-layer")))
    deformation_dimension = raw.get("deformation_dimension")
    equation_dimension = raw.get("equation_dimension")
    operator_values = raw.get("operator")
    if not isinstance(operator_values, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: operator must be a list")
    if not isinstance(deformation_dimension, int):
        deformation_dimension = len(operator_values&#91;0&#93;) if operator_values else None
    if not isinstance(equation_dimension, int):
        equation_dimension = len(operator_values)
    if not isinstance(deformation_dimension, int) or deformation_dimension &lt; 0:
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: deformation_dimension is required")
    if equation_dimension &lt; 0:
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: equation_dimension must be nonnegative")
    operator = parse_matrix(
        field,
        operator_values,
        rows=equation_dimension,
        columns=deformation_dimension,
        name=f"{identifier}.operator",
    )
    deformation_basis = &#91;
        str(value)
        for value in raw.get(
            "deformation_basis",
            &#91;f"e{index}" for index in range(deformation_dimension)&#93;,
        )
    &#93;
    equation_basis = &#91;
        str(value)
        for value in raw.get(
            "equation_basis",
            &#91;f"w{index}" for index in range(equation_dimension)&#93;,
        )
    &#93;
    if len(deformation_basis) != deformation_dimension:
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: deformation_basis has the wrong length")
    if len(equation_basis) != equation_dimension:
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: equation_basis has the wrong length")

    kernel_basis = nullspace(
        operator,
        rows=equation_dimension,
        columns=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    left_kernel_basis = left_nullspace(
        operator,
        rows=equation_dimension,
        columns=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )

    raw_actions = raw.get("actions", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_actions, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: actions must be a list")
    actions: dict&#91;str, ActionSpace&#93; = {}
    for index, action in enumerate(raw_actions):
        if not isinstance(action, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: action {index} must be an object")
        parsed = _parse_action(
            field,
            action,
            identifier=identifier,
            index=index,
            deformation_dimension=deformation_dimension,
            operator=operator,
        )
        if parsed.name in actions:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}: duplicate action name {parsed.name!r}"
            )
        actions&#91;parsed.name&#93; = parsed

    for action in actions.values():
        if action.parent is None:
            continue
        if action.parent not in actions:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}.{action.name}: unknown parent {action.parent!r}"
            )
        for generator in action.basis:
            if not vector_in_span(
                generator,
                actions&#91;action.parent&#93;.basis,
                ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
                field=field,
            ):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{identifier}.{action.name} is not contained in "
                    f"parent {action.parent}"
                )

    raw_recharts = raw.get("recharts", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_recharts, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: recharts must be a list")
    recharts: list&#91;RechartSpace&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    rechart_names: set&#91;str&#93; = set()
    for index, rechart in enumerate(raw_recharts):
        if not isinstance(rechart, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: rechart {index} must be an object")
        name = str(rechart.get("name", f"rechart-{index}"))
        if name in rechart_names:
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: duplicate rechart name {name!r}")
        rechart_names.add(name)
        values = rechart.get("generators", &#91;&#93;)
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}.{name}: generators must be a list")
        labels, generators = parse_generators(
            field,
            values,
            width=deformation_dimension,
            name=f"{identifier}.{name}",
        )
        for generator_index, generator in enumerate(generators):
            if any(matrix_vector_product(operator, generator, field)):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{identifier}.{name}: rechart generator {generator_index} "
                    "is not in ker(D)"
                )
        recharts.append(
            RechartSpace(
                name,
                labels,
                generators,
                _basis_from_generators(
                    generators,
                    ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
                    field=field,
                ),
            )
        )

    gauge_actions = raw.get("gauge_actions")
    if gauge_actions is None:
        gauge_action = raw.get("gauge_action")
        gauge_actions = &#91;&#93; if gauge_action is None else &#91;gauge_action&#93;
    if not isinstance(gauge_actions, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: gauge_actions must be a list")
    gauge_actions = &#91;str(name) for name in gauge_actions&#93;
    for name in gauge_actions:
        if name not in actions:
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: unknown gauge action {name!r}")

    forcing: Vector | None = None
    if "forcing" in raw:
        if not isinstance(raw&#91;"forcing"&#93;, list):
            raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: forcing must be a vector")
        forcing = parse_vector(
            field,
            raw&#91;"forcing"&#93;,
            width=equation_dimension,
            name=f"{identifier}.forcing",
        )

    raw_functionals = raw.get("obstruction_functionals", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_functionals, list):
        raise ContractError(
            f"{identifier}: obstruction_functionals must be a list"
        )
    obstruction_functionals = &#91;
        parse_vector(
            field,
            value.get("vector") if isinstance(value, Mapping) else value,
            width=equation_dimension,
            name=f"{identifier}.obstruction_functionals&#91;{index}&#93;",
        )
        for index, value in enumerate(raw_functionals)
    &#93;
    for index, functional in enumerate(obstruction_functionals):
        if any(row_matrix_product(functional, operator, field)):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{identifier}: obstruction functional {index} is not left-null"
            )

    gauge_basis = _basis_from_generators(
        &#91;
            generator
            for name in gauge_actions
            for generator in actions&#91;name&#93;.basis
        &#93;,
        ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    rechart_generators = &#91;
        generator
        for rechart in recharts
        for generator in rechart.basis
    &#93;
    explained_basis, rechart_added = independent_extension(
        gauge_basis,
        rechart_generators,
        ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    _, quotient_representatives = independent_extension(
        explained_basis,
        kernel_basis,
        ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )

    action_result: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for action in actions.values():
        entry: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
            "name": action.name,
            "role": action.role,
            "parent": action.parent,
            "input_form": action.input_form,
            "source_dimension": action.source_dimension,
            "source_basis": action.source_basis,
            "source_stabilizer_dimension": len(action.source_stabilizer_basis),
            "generator_count": len(action.generators),
            "rank": len(action.basis),
            "rank_nullity_verified": True,
            "contained_in_kernel": True,
            "parent_inclusion_verified": True,
        }
        if include_vectors:
            entry&#91;"action_matrix"&#93; = _serialize_matrix(
                field,
                action.action_matrix,
            )
            entry&#91;"source_stabilizer_basis"&#93; = &#91;
                serialize_vector(field, vector)
                for vector in action.source_stabilizer_basis
            &#93;
            entry&#91;"basis"&#93; = &#91;
                serialize_vector(field, vector)
                for vector in action.basis
            &#93;
        action_result.append(entry)

    rechart_result: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    cumulative = list(gauge_basis)
    for rechart in recharts:
        cumulative, added = independent_extension(
            cumulative,
            rechart.basis,
            ambient_dimension=deformation_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        entry: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
            "name": rechart.name,
            "generator_count": len(rechart.generators),
            "rank": len(rechart.basis),
            "increment_after_previous_spaces": len(added),
            "contained_in_kernel": True,
        }
        if include_vectors:
            entry&#91;"basis"&#93; = &#91;
                serialize_vector(field, vector)
                for vector in rechart.basis
            &#93;
        rechart_result.append(entry)

    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "id": identifier,
        "deformation_dimension": deformation_dimension,
        "equation_dimension": equation_dimension,
        "operator_rank": deformation_dimension - len(kernel_basis),
        "kernel_dimension": len(kernel_basis),
        "cokernel_dimension": len(left_kernel_basis),
        "actions": action_result,
        "gauge_actions": gauge_actions,
        "gauge_dimension": len(gauge_basis),
        "recharts": rechart_result,
        "rechart_increment": len(rechart_added),
        "explained_dimension": len(explained_basis),
        "unexplained_dimension": len(kernel_basis) - len(explained_basis),
        "obstruction_functional_count": len(obstruction_functionals),
        "obstruction_functionals_verified": True,
    }
    if forcing is not None:
        result&#91;"forcing_pairings"&#93; = &#91;
            field.serialize(dot(functional, forcing, field))
            for functional in obstruction_functionals
        &#93;
    if include_vectors:
        result&#91;"kernel_basis"&#93; = &#91;
            serialize_vector(field, vector)
            for vector in kernel_basis
        &#93;
        result&#91;"left_kernel_basis"&#93; = &#91;
            serialize_vector(field, vector)
            for vector in left_kernel_basis
        &#93;
        result&#91;"gauge_basis"&#93; = &#91;
            serialize_vector(field, vector)
            for vector in gauge_basis
        &#93;
        result&#91;"explained_basis"&#93; = &#91;
            serialize_vector(field, vector)
            for vector in explained_basis
        &#93;
    result&#91;"quotient_representatives"&#93; = &#91;
        serialize_vector(field, vector)
        for vector in quotient_representatives
    &#93;

    metadata = raw.get("metadata", {})
    if not isinstance(metadata, Mapping):
        raise ContractError(f"{identifier}: metadata must be an object")
    data = LayerData(
        identifier=identifier,
        deformation_dimension=deformation_dimension,
        equation_dimension=equation_dimension,
        operator=operator,
        kernel_basis=kernel_basis,
        left_kernel_basis=left_kernel_basis,
        actions=actions,
        recharts=recharts,
        gauge_actions=gauge_actions,
        gauge_basis=gauge_basis,
        explained_basis=explained_basis,
        quotient_representatives=quotient_representatives,
        forcing=forcing,
        obstruction_functionals=obstruction_functionals,
        deformation_basis=deformation_basis,
        equation_basis=equation_basis,
        metadata=metadata,
    )
    return LayerAudit(data, result)

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/linear.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Dependency-free exact linear algebra over a supplied field."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .fields import ContractError, ExactField

Vector = list&#91;Any&#93;
Matrix = list&#91;Vector&#93;


def parse_vector(
    field: ExactField,
    values: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;,
    *,
    width: int,
    name: str,
) -&gt; Vector:
    vector = &#91;field.parse(value) for value in values&#93;
    if len(vector) != width:
        raise ContractError(f"{name} has length {len(vector)}, expected {width}")
    return vector


def parse_matrix(
    field: ExactField,
    values: Sequence&#91;Sequence&#91;Any&#93;&#93;,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    name: str,
) -&gt; Matrix:
    if len(values) != rows:
        raise ContractError(f"{name} has {len(values)} rows, expected {rows}")
    return &#91;
        parse_vector(
            field,
            row,
            width=columns,
            name=f"{name}&#91;{index}&#93;",
        )
        for index, row in enumerate(values)
    &#93;


def transpose(matrix: Matrix, *, rows: int, columns: int) -&gt; Matrix:
    if len(matrix) != rows or any(len(row) != columns for row in matrix):
        raise ContractError("matrix shape does not match declared dimensions")
    return &#91;
        &#91;matrix&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93; for row in range(rows)&#93;
        for column in range(columns)
    &#93;


def matrix_vector_product(
    matrix: Matrix,
    vector: Vector,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; Vector:
    return &#91;
        sum(
            (entry * value for entry, value in zip(row, vector)),
            field.zero,
        )
        for row in matrix
    &#93;


def row_matrix_product(
    row: Vector,
    matrix: Matrix,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; Vector:
    if matrix and len(row) != len(matrix):
        raise ContractError("row/matrix dimensions do not agree")
    columns = len(matrix&#91;0&#93;) if matrix else 0
    return &#91;
        sum(
            (
                row&#91;index&#93; * matrix&#91;index&#93;&#91;column&#93;
                for index in range(len(row))
            ),
            field.zero,
        )
        for column in range(columns)
    &#93;


def matrix_product(
    left: Matrix,
    right: Matrix,
    *,
    left_rows: int,
    middle: int,
    right_columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; Matrix:
    if len(left) != left_rows or any(len(row) != middle for row in left):
        raise ContractError("left matrix has the wrong shape")
    if len(right) != middle or any(
        len(row) != right_columns for row in right
    ):
        raise ContractError("right matrix has the wrong shape")
    return &#91;
        &#91;
            sum(
                (
                    left&#91;row&#93;&#91;index&#93; * right&#91;index&#93;&#91;column&#93;
                    for index in range(middle)
                ),
                field.zero,
            )
            for column in range(right_columns)
        &#93;
        for row in range(left_rows)
    &#93;


def rref(
    matrix: Matrix,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;Matrix, list&#91;int&#93;&#93;:
    if len(matrix) != rows or any(len(row) != columns for row in matrix):
        raise ContractError("RREF input shape does not match declared dimensions")
    result = &#91;row&#91;:&#93; for row in matrix&#93;
    pivots: list&#91;int&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    pivot_row = 0
    for column in range(columns):
        pivot = next(
            (
                row
                for row in range(pivot_row, rows)
                if result&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93;
            ),
            None,
        )
        if pivot is None:
            continue
        result&#91;pivot_row&#93;, result&#91;pivot&#93; = result&#91;pivot&#93;, result&#91;pivot_row&#93;
        scale = result&#91;pivot_row&#93;&#91;column&#93;
        result&#91;pivot_row&#93; = &#91;entry / scale for entry in result&#91;pivot_row&#93;&#93;
        for row in range(rows):
            if row == pivot_row:
                continue
            factor = result&#91;row&#93;&#91;column&#93;
            if factor:
                result&#91;row&#93; = &#91;
                    left - factor * right
                    for left, right in zip(result&#91;row&#93;, result&#91;pivot_row&#93;)
                &#93;
        pivots.append(column)
        pivot_row += 1
        if pivot_row == rows:
            break
    return result, pivots


def rank(
    matrix: Matrix,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; int:
    return len(
        rref(matrix, rows=rows, columns=columns, field=field)&#91;1&#93;
    )


def nullspace(
    matrix: Matrix,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; list&#91;Vector&#93;:
    reduced, pivots = rref(
        matrix,
        rows=rows,
        columns=columns,
        field=field,
    )
    pivot_rows = {column: row for row, column in enumerate(pivots)}
    free_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(columns) if column not in pivot_rows
    &#93;
    basis: list&#91;Vector&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for free_column in free_columns:
        vector = &#91;field.zero for _ in range(columns)&#93;
        vector&#91;free_column&#93; = field.one
        for pivot_column, row in pivot_rows.items():
            vector&#91;pivot_column&#93; = -reduced&#91;row&#93;&#91;free_column&#93;
        basis.append(vector)
    return basis


def left_nullspace(
    matrix: Matrix,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; list&#91;Vector&#93;:
    return nullspace(
        transpose(matrix, rows=rows, columns=columns),
        rows=columns,
        columns=rows,
        field=field,
    )


def solve_affine(
    matrix: Matrix,
    rhs: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;,
    *,
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;bool, Vector | None&#93;:
    """Solve ``matrix * x = rhs`` with all free coordinates set to zero.

    The returned particular solution is deterministic because the package's
    RREF pivot convention is deterministic.  ``None`` denotes inconsistency.
    The homogeneous direction space is obtained independently with
    :func:`nullspace`.
    """

    if len(rhs) != rows:
        raise ContractError(
            f"affine right-hand side has length {len(rhs)}, expected {rows}"
        )
    if len(matrix) != rows or any(len(row) != columns for row in matrix):
        raise ContractError("affine matrix shape does not match dimensions")
    augmented = &#91;
        &#91;*matrix&#91;row&#93;, rhs&#91;row&#93;&#93;
        for row in range(rows)
    &#93;
    reduced, pivots = rref(
        augmented,
        rows=rows,
        columns=columns + 1,
        field=field,
    )
    if columns in pivots:
        return False, None

    solution = &#91;field.zero for _ in range(columns)&#93;
    for pivot_row, pivot_column in enumerate(pivots):
        if pivot_column &gt;= columns:
            return False, None
        solution&#91;pivot_column&#93; = reduced&#91;pivot_row&#93;&#91;columns&#93;
    if matrix_vector_product(matrix, solution, field) != list(rhs):
        raise ContractError("internal affine-solver reconstruction failed")
    return True, solution


def vectors_to_column_matrix(
    vectors: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    *,
    ambient_dimension: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; Matrix:
    if any(len(vector) != ambient_dimension for vector in vectors):
        raise ContractError("vector dimensions do not agree")
    return &#91;
        &#91;vector&#91;row&#93; for vector in vectors&#93;
        for row in range(ambient_dimension)
    &#93;


def rank_of_vectors(
    vectors: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    *,
    ambient_dimension: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; int:
    if not vectors:
        return 0
    matrix = vectors_to_column_matrix(
        vectors,
        ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    return rank(
        matrix,
        rows=ambient_dimension,
        columns=len(vectors),
        field=field,
    )


def independent_extension(
    base: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    candidates: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    *,
    ambient_dimension: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;list&#91;Vector&#93;, list&#91;Vector&#93;&#93;:
    accepted = list(base)
    current_rank = rank_of_vectors(
        accepted,
        ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    added: list&#91;Vector&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for candidate in candidates:
        trial = accepted + &#91;candidate&#93;
        trial_rank = rank_of_vectors(
            trial,
            ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        if trial_rank &gt; current_rank:
            accepted.append(candidate)
            added.append(candidate)
            current_rank = trial_rank
    return accepted, added


def vector_in_span(
    vector: Vector,
    span: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    *,
    ambient_dimension: int,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; bool:
    return rank_of_vectors(
        &#91;*span, vector&#93;,
        ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
        field=field,
    ) == rank_of_vectors(
        span,
        ambient_dimension=ambient_dimension,
        field=field,
    )


def parse_generators(
    field: ExactField,
    values: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;,
    *,
    width: int,
    name: str,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;list&#91;str&#93;, list&#91;Vector&#93;&#93;:
    labels: list&#91;str&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    vectors: list&#91;Vector&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for index, value in enumerate(values):
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            label = str(value.get("name", f"{name}-{index}"))
            raw_vector = value.get("vector")
        else:
            label = f"{name}-{index}"
            raw_vector = value
        if not isinstance(raw_vector, list):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}&#91;{index}&#93; must be a vector or named vector"
            )
        labels.append(label)
        vectors.append(
            parse_vector(
                field,
                raw_vector,
                width=width,
                name=f"{name}&#91;{index}&#93;",
            )
        )
    return labels, vectors


def serialize_vector(field: ExactField, vector: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;) -&gt; list&#91;Any&#93;:
    return &#91;field.serialize(value) for value in vector&#93;


def dot(
    left: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;,
    right: Sequence&#91;Any&#93;,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; Any:
    return sum((a * b for a, b in zip(left, right)), field.zero)
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi,
)


class Program5AlgebraicFourthOrderKuranishiTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_complete_algebraic_cubic_lift_fibre_is_retained(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"coefficient_field"&#93;, "Q(sqrt(-3))")
        self.assertTrue(
            result&#91;"conjugate_result_follows_by_Q_galois_symmetry"&#93;
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;, 5)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;, 5)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_lift_affine_dimension"&#93;, 17)

        order_four = result&#91;"order_four_map"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(order_four&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 17)
        self.assertEqual(order_four&#91;"linear_coefficient_count"&#93;, 17)
        self.assertEqual(
            order_four&#91;"diagonal_quadratic_coefficient_count"&#93;,
            17,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            order_four&#91;"off_diagonal_quadratic_coefficient_count"&#93;,
            136,
        )
        self.assertGreater(order_four&#91;"active_target_coordinate_count"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"&#93;,
            order_four&#91;
                "constant_term_outside_variable_coefficient_span"
            &#93;,
        )
        if result&#91;"intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"&#93;:
            self.assertGreater(
                order_four&#91;"constant_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                order_four&#91;"variable_coefficient_rank"&#93;,
            )
            certificate = result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;
            self.assertIsNotNone(certificate)
            self.assertNotEqual(
                certificate&#91;"pairing_with_constant_term"&#93;,
                "0",
            )
            self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)
        else:
            self.assertEqual(
                order_four&#91;"constant_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                order_four&#91;"variable_coefficient_rank"&#93;,
            )
            self.assertIsNone(result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "audited_ratio": result&#91;"audited_ratio"&#93;,
                    "conjugate_ratio": result&#91;"conjugate_ratio"&#93;,
                    "cubic_effect_rank": result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                    "cubic_lift_affine_dimension": result&#91;
                        "cubic_lift_affine_dimension"
                    &#93;,
                    "constant_nonzero_count": order_four&#91;
                        "constant_nonzero_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "active_target_coordinate_count": order_four&#91;
                        "active_target_coordinate_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "variable_coefficient_rank": order_four&#91;
                        "variable_coefficient_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "constant_augmented_rank": order_four&#91;
                        "constant_augmented_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "intrinsic_order_four_obstruction": result&#91;
                        "intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"
                    &#93;,
                    "certificate_pairing": (
                        result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93; or {}
                    ).get("pairing_with_constant_term"),
                    "certificate_nonzero_count": (
                        result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93; or {}
                    ).get("nonzero_count"),
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_exceptional_finite_lines.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_exceptional_finite_lines import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_exceptional_finite_lines,
)


class Program5ExceptionalFiniteLineTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_pairing_ideal_roots_are_specialized_exactly(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_exceptional_finite_lines()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"exceptional_ratio_count"&#93;, 3)
        ratios = result&#91;"exceptional_ratios"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(ratios), 3)
        self.assertTrue(result&#91;"conjugate_rank_profiles_equal"&#93;)
        for item in ratios:
            self.assertEqual(
                item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;
                == item&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
            )
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"all_exceptional_finite_ratios_obstructed"&#93;,
            all(not item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93; for item in ratios),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"finite_selected_plane_closed_at_cubic_order"&#93;,
            result&#91;"all_exceptional_finite_ratios_obstructed"&#93;,
        )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "generic_pairing_polynomial": result&#91;
                        "generic_pairing_polynomial"
                    &#93;,
                    "exceptional_ratios": &#91;
                        {
                            "label": item&#91;"label"&#93;,
                            "field": item&#91;"field"&#93;,
                            "rank": item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                            "augmented_rank": item&#91;
                                "cubic_augmented_rank"
                            &#93;,
                            "solvable": item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                        }
                        for item in ratios
                    &#93;,
                    "finite_selected_plane_closed": result&#91;
                        "finite_selected_plane_closed_at_cubic_order"
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,

                                "cubic_augmented_rank"
                            &#93;,
                            "solvable": sample&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                        }
                        for sample in result&#91;"samples"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_fourth_order_kuranishi.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_fourth_order_kuranishi import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi,
)


class Program5FourthOrderKuranishiTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_complete_cubic_lift_fibre_is_retained_at_order_four(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_lift_affine_dimension"&#93;, 20)
        self.assertGreater(
            result&#91;"deterministic_quartic_residual"&#93;&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;,
            0,
        )

        kuranishi = result&#91;"quartic_kuranishi"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(kuranishi&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 20)
        self.assertGreater(kuranishi&#91;"target_coefficient_rank"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            kuranishi&#91;"independent_equation_count"&#93;,
            len(kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;),
        )
        for equation in kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;:
            self.assertLessEqual(equation&#91;"total_degree"&#93;, 2)
            self.assertGreater(len(equation&#91;"terms"&#93;), 0)

        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;,
            result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93; is not None,
        )
        if result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;:
            self.assertTrue(result&#91;"rational_zero_verified"&#93;)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(
                result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93;&#91;"coefficient_count"&#93;,
                0,
            )
        else:
            self.assertFalse(result&#91;"rational_zero_verified"&#93;)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "cubic_effect_rank": result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                    "cubic_lift_affine_dimension": result&#91;
                        "cubic_lift_affine_dimension"
                    &#93;,
                    "deterministic_quartic_residual_nonzero_count": result&#91;
                        "deterministic_quartic_residual"
                    &#93;&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;,
                    "quartic_target_coefficient_rank": kuranishi&#91;
                        "target_coefficient_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "quartic_equations": &#91;
                        {
                            "row_variable": equation&#91;
                                "schur_row_variable"
                            &#93;,
                            "column_monomial": equation&#91;
                                "schur_column_monomial"
                            &#93;,
                            "expression": equation&#91;"expression"&#93;,
                            "term_count": len(equation&#91;"terms"&#93;),
                        }
                        for equation in kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                    "rational_zero_found": result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;,
                    "rational_zero": result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_generic_line_obstruction.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_generic_line_obstruction import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_generic_line_obstruction,
)


class Program5GenericLineObstructionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_finite_ratio_obstruction_is_computed_symbolically(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_generic_line_obstruction()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertTrue(result&#91;"second_order_residual_identically_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"left_kernel_dimension_over_Q(r)"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"generic_augmented_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93;,
            result&#91;"generic_effect_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93; + 1,
        )

        basis = result&#91;"primitive_left_kernel_basis"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(basis), 2)
        for witness in basis:
            self.assertEqual(witness&#91;"coordinate_gcd"&#93;, "1")
            self.assertGreater(witness&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        pairing_ideal = result&#91;"pairing_ideal"&#93;
        self.assertNotEqual(pairing_ideal&#91;"generator"&#93;, "0")
        certificate = result&#91;"combined_bezout_certificate"&#93;
        self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_cubic_residual"&#93;, "0")
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"&#93;,
            pairing_ideal&#91;"degree"&#93; == 0,
        )

        samples = result&#91;"sample_checks"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(samples), 9)
        for sample in samples:
            self.assertFalse(sample&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;)
            self.assertGreater(
                sample&#91;"augmented_rank"&#93;,
                sample&#91;"effect_rank"&#93;,
            )
        if result&#91;"universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"&#93;:
            self.assertEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_degree"&#93;, 0)
            for sample in samples:
                self.assertNotEqual(sample&#91;"combined_pairing"&#93;, 0)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "compressed_cubic_equation_count": result&#91;
                        "compressed_cubic_equation_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "effect_rank": result&#91;"generic_effect_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93;,
                    "augmented_rank": result&#91;
                        "generic_augmented_rank_over_Q(r)"
                    &#93;,
                    "left_kernel_dimension": result&#91;
                        "left_kernel_dimension_over_Q(r)"
                    &#93;,
                    "basis_pairings": &#91;
                        witness&#91;"pairing_with_cubic_residual"&#93;
                        for witness in basis
                    &#93;,
                    "pairing_ideal_generator": pairing_ideal&#91;"generator"&#93;,
                    "pairing_ideal_degree": pairing_ideal&#91;"degree"&#93;,
                    "combined_witness_nonzero_count": certificate&#91;
                        "nonzero_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "combined_witness_sha256": certificate&#91;"sha256"&#93;,
                    "combined_pairing": certificate&#91;
                        "pairing_with_cubic_residual"
                    &#93;,
                    "universal": result&#91;
                        "universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_infinity_fibre_formal_scan import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan,
)


class Program5InfinityFibreFormalScanTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_coordinate_samples_are_lifted_through_order_four(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan(max_order=4)

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"infinity_fibre_dimension"&#93;, 18)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"sample_count"&#93;, 19)
        self.assertEqual(len(result&#91;"samples"&#93;), 19)

        by_name = {item&#91;"name"&#93;: item for item in result&#91;"samples"&#93;}
        base = by_name&#91;"infinity:base"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 4)
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(
            base&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;,
            "1/2",
        )

        xi5 = by_name&#91;"infinity:+xi_5"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(xi5&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(xi5&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 2)

        for sample in result&#91;"samples"&#93;:
            obstruction = sample&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;
            if obstruction is None:
                self.assertEqual(sample&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 4)
            else:
                self.assertEqual(
                    sample&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;,
                    obstruction - 1,
                )
                self.assertNotEqual(
                    sample&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;,
                    0,
                )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "first_obstruction_histogram": result&#91;
                        "first_obstruction_histogram"
                    &#93;,
                    "survivor_count": result&#91;"survivor_count"&#93;,
                    "survivors": result&#91;
                        "survivors_through_requested_order"
                    &#93;,
                    "profiles": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": sample&#91;"name"&#93;,
                            "maximum_compatible_order": sample&#91;
                                "maximum_compatible_order"
                            &#93;,
                            "first_obstructed_order": sample&#91;
                                "first_obstructed_order"
                            &#93;,
                            "fixed_effect_rank": sample&#91;
                                "fixed_tangent_effect_rank"
                            &#93;,
                            "pairing": sample.get(
                                "obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                        }
                        for sample in result&#91;"samples"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

from __future__ import annotations

import json

    analyze_line_formal_arc,
)


class Program5RankSixLineFormalArcTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_theta_u_is_lifted_until_an_exact_obstruction_or_order_eight(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_line_formal_arc(
            ratio=None,
            max_order=8,
            verify_effect=True,
        )

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"direction"&#93;, "theta_u")
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"u_over_v"&#93;, "infinity")
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)

        first_obstructed = result&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;
        if first_obstructed is None:
            self.assertEqual(result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 8)
        else:
            self.assertGreaterEqual(first_obstructed, 4)
            self.assertEqual(
                result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;,

            )
            certificate = result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;
            self.assertEqual(certificate&#91;"order"&#93;, first_obstructed)
            self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;, 0)
            self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "fixed_tangent_effect_rank": result&#91;
                        "fixed_tangent_effect_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "maximum_compatible_order": result&#91;
                        "maximum_compatible_order"
                    &#93;,
                    "first_obstructed_order": first_obstructed,
                    "order_data": &#91;
                        {
                            "order": item&#91;"order"&#93;,
                            "compatible": item&#91;"compatible"&#93;,
                            "effect_rank": item.get("effect_rank"),
                            "augmented_rank": item.get("augmented_rank"),
                            "tangent_adjustment_count": item.get(
                                "tangent_adjustment", {}
                            ).get("coefficient_count"),
                            "correction_nonzero_count": item.get(
                                "finalized_correction", {}
                            ).get("nonzero_count"),
                            "pairing": item.get(
                                "obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                        }
                        for item in result&#91;"orders"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_rank_six_line_scan.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_line_scan import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_line_scan,
)


class Program5RankSixLineScanTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_selected_projective_lines_are_audited_exactly(self) -&gt; None:
        ratios = (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5)
        result = analyze_line_scan(ratios=ratios, verify_axes=True)

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"ambient_operation_dimension"&#93;, 115)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)

        directions = result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93; + &#91;result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(directions), len(ratios) + 1)
        for direction in directions:
            self.assertTrue(direction&#91;"second_order_compatible"&#93;)
            self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension"&#93;, 22)
            self.assertEqual(
                direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                direction&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;
                == direction&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
            )
            if direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;:
                self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)
                self.assertIsNotNone(
                    direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solution_free_dimension"&#93;
                )
            else:
                self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 3)
                certificate = direction&#91;"cubic_obstruction_certificate"&#93;
                self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;, 0)
                self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        ratio_zero = next(
            item
            for item in result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93;
            if item&#91;"u_over_v"&#93; == 0
        )
        self.assertEqual(ratio_zero&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;,
            22,
        )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "finite": &#91;
                        {
                            "u_over_v": str(item&#91;"u_over_v"&#93;),
                            "rank": item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                            "augmented_rank": item&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                            "solvable": item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                            "pairing": item.get(
                                "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                            "witness_sha256": item.get(
                                "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("witness_sha256"),
                        }
                        for item in result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                    "infinity": {
                        "rank": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                        "augmented_rank": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;
                            "cubic_augmented_rank"
                        &#93;,
                        "solvable": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;
                            "cubic_lift_solvable"
                        &#93;,
                        "pairing": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;.get(
                            "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                        ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                    },
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/tests/test_adapters.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_row_killing import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_packet,
)
from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program6_legacy import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_legacy_document,
)


class AdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_program6_legacy_k4_shape(self) -&gt; None:
        legacy = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "name": "legacy synthetic k4",
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "label": "layer 1",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 2",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0, 0&#93;, &#91;0, 1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 3",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 4",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "support_transport": {"k": 4, "monomials": &#91;&#91;2, 2&#93;&#93;},
                },
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_legacy_document(legacy)
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(
            &#91;layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93; for layer in report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#93;,
            &#91;1, 2, 1, 1&#93;,
        )
        self.assertEqual(report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;3&#93;&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;3&#93;&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
        closure = report&#91;"adapter_metadata"&#93;&#91;"support_closures"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"closure"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(closure, &#91;&#91;2, 2&#93;, &#91;-2, 1&#93;, &#91;-6, 0&#93;&#93;)

    def test_program5_template(self) -&gt; None:
        packet = json.loads(
            (HERE.parent / "examples" / "program5_row_killing_template.json").read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
        report = analyze_packet(packet)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 4)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;"-1"&#93;)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/tests/test_examples.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex import analyze_document  # noqa: E402


class ExampleTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_two_chart_example_is_current(self) -&gt; None:
        document = json.loads(
            (HERE.parent / "examples" / "two_chart_rational.json").read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
        report = analyze_document(document)
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_transitions_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(
            &#91;layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93; for layer in report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#93;,
            &#91;1, 1&#93;,
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/tests/test_filtered_operation_complex.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent.parent
EXAMPLE = HERE.parent / "examples" / "two_chart_rational.json"
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from filtered_operation_complex import ContractError, analyze_document  # noqa: E402


class FilteredOperationComplexTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def rational_contract(self) -&gt; dict:
        return json.loads(EXAMPLE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_rational_hierarchy_rechart_and_transport(self) -&gt; None:
        report = analyze_document(self.rational_contract())
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_transitions_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertFalse(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertFalse(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        for layer in report&#91;"layers"&#93;:
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 3)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"cokernel_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;"1"&#93;)
            self.assertFalse(layer&#91;"forcing_solvable"&#93;)
            self.assertIsNone(layer&#91;"affine_solution_dimension"&#93;)
            self.assertEqual(
                layer&#91;"complete_left_null_forcing_pairings"&#93;,
                &#91;"1"&#93;,
            )

            actions = {entry&#91;"name"&#93;: entry for entry in layer&#91;"actions"&#93;}
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (4, 3, 1),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (3, 2, 1),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (2, 1, 1),
            )

        transition = report&#91;"transitions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(transition&#91;"operation_map_checks"&#93;), 3)
        self.assertTrue(
            all(
                item&#91;"action_square_verified"&#93;
                and item&#91;"source_stabilizer_transport_verified"&#93;
                for item in transition&#91;"operation_map_checks"&#93;
            )
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            transition&#91;"rechart_span_checks"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"transported_rank"&#93;,
            1,
        )
        quotient = transition&#91;"true_quotient_check"&#93;
        self.assertTrue(quotient&#91;"explained_space_transport_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(quotient&#91;"induced_quotient_rank"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertTrue(quotient&#91;"isomorphism_required"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(
            transition&#91;"forcing_check"&#93;&#91;"dual_pairings_preserved"&#93;
        )
        self.assertEqual(transition&#91;"forcing_check"&#93;&#91;"pairings"&#93;, &#91;"1"&#93;)

    def test_solvable_affine_forcing_returns_particular_solution(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {"kind": "rational"},
            "report_options": {"include_vectors": True},
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "solvable-affine-layer",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "forcing": &#91;-2&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_document(contract)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(layer&#91;"forcing_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(layer&#91;"forcing_compatibility_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"affine_solution_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"particular_solution"&#93;, &#91;"2", "0"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"complete_left_null_forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;&#93;)

    def test_generator_form_also_reports_source_stabilizer(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {"kind": "rational"},
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "dependent-generators",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "actions": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": "filtered",
                            "role": "filtered",
                            "generators": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;, &#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                        }
                    &#93;,
                    "gauge_actions": &#91;"filtered"&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        action = analyze_document(contract)&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"input_form"&#93;, "generators")
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"rank"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;, 1)

    def test_number_field_contract(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {
                "kind": "number_field",
                "modulus": &#91;-2, 0, 1&#93;,
                "symbol": "u",
            },
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "quadratic-field",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;, &#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;&#93;,
                    "actions": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": "filtered",
                            "role": "filtered",
                            "generators": &#91;&#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;, &#91;0, -1&#93;&#93;&#93;,
                        }
                    &#93;,
                    "gauge_actions": &#91;"filtered"&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_document(contract)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertIsNone(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(
            layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
            0,
        )

    def test_rejects_non_kernel_operation(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;2&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;0&#93; = 1
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_rejects_false_parent_inclusion(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93; = {
            "name": "formal",
            "role": "formal",
            "source_dimension": 1,
            "source_basis": &#91;"F0"&#93;,
            "action_matrix": &#91;&#91;0&#93;, &#91;1&#93;, &#91;0&#93;, &#91;0&#93;&#93;,
        }
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_rejects_false_operation_transport_square(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"transitions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"operation_map_pairs"&#93;&#91;2&#93;&#91;"source_map"&#93; = &#91;
            &#91;0, 0&#93;,
            &#91;0, 0&#93;,
        &#93;
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_action_requires_exactly_one_input_form(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"generators"&#93; = &#91;&#91;0, 1, 0, 0&#93;&#93;
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_json_roundtrip(self) -&gt; None:
        document = json.loads(json.dumps(self.rational_contract()))
        self.assertEqual(analyze_document(document)&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>
